# <span class="deva-large">त</span> <span class="iast-large">ta</span>

<div class="dict-page-header">
  <span class="badge-count">751 entrées</span>
  <a href="../" class="back-link">← Index du dictionnaire</a>
</div>

---

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--t_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰तृ</span> <i>-tṛ</i> forme des noms d'agent ou de parenté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰त</span> <i>-ta</i> forme des a. (not. des pp.).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--taa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰ता</span> <i>-tā</i> forme des sf. d'état; qualité de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. -té.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰तम</span> <i>-tama</i> sf. forme des super. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> forme des a. ord. (ième) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (rare) après nom, cf. <a href="#entry-ka_nvatama" class="skt-link">kaṇvatama</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>-tamām</i> intens. après adv., qqf. verbes.<br> <i>2000</i> <i>tama</i> <i>varṣa</i> l'an 2000.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰तर</span> <i>-tara</i> sf. forme des a. compar. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qqf. sf. de nom, cf. <a href="#entry-v_rtratara" class="skt-link">vṛtratara</a> — ind. <i>-tarām</i> intens. après prép., cf. <a href="#entry-atitaraam" class="skt-link">atitarām</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adv. (plus tardif) intens. après verbe; (avec <a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>) pas du tout.<br> <i>pacatitarām</i> il cuit mieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰तस्</span> <i>-tas</i> forme des adv. de lieu ou de circonstance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> forme des adv. de manière signifiant «selon», «par suite de», «à la manière de», «respectivement à», etc. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sf. ablatif.<br> <i>mumbaitaḥ</i> <i>āgatavat</i> il vient de Bombay.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰तवत्</span> <i>-tavat</i> forme des ppa. (cf. <a href="#entry-ktavatu" class="skt-link">ktavatu</a>)
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰तव्य</span> <i>-tavya</i> gram. forme des pfp. d'obligation ou de nécessité (cf. <a href="#entry-tavyat" class="skt-link">tavyat</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--taya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰तय</span> <i>-taya</i> sf. forme l'adjectif n-partite après le nombre n (f. en <i>-tayī</i>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰ति</span> <i>-ti</i> (primaire) forme des s. f. d'action <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] dénote plutôt l'objet résultant de l'action <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (num.) forme des dizaines.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰तिन्</span> <i>-tin</i> cf. <a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰त्र</span> <i>-tra</i> forme des adv. de lieu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> forme l'instrument n. utilisé par l'agt. en <a href="#entry--t_r" class="skt-link">-tṛ</a> corr.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰त्त</span> <i>-tta</i> (pour <i>-datta</i>) après préverbe, dont le ‘<i>i</i>’ final éventuel est allongé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰तु</span> <i>-tu</i> forme des s. d'action ou d'instrument, et des a. indiquant la possession.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tum">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰तुम्</span> <i>-tum</i> forme l'infinitif (sens dat. de «pour faire»).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰त्व</span> <i>-tva</i> forme des s. n. d'état; essence de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰त्वा</span> <i>-tvā</i> forme des abs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--tya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰त्य</span> <i>-tya</i> forme des a. à partir de particules.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृ</span> <i>tṛ</i> cf. <a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_n_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृण्ण</span> <i>tṛṇṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-t_rd" class="skt-link">tṛd</a>] a. <a href="#entry-t_r_n_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-t_r_n_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-t_r_n_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tṛṇṇā</i> percé; fendu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृण</span> <i>tṛṇa</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-taru_na" class="skt-link">taruṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-t_r_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> herbe, herbage, graminée; brin d'herbe; paille, fétu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chose insignifiante ou négligeable.<br> <i>na</i> <i>tvā</i> <i>tṛṇāya</i> <i>manye</i> (méprisant) je me soucis de toi comme d'un fétu de paille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_naavarta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tṛṇāvarta</i> [<a href="#entry-aavarta" class="skt-link">āvarta</a>] <a href="#entry-t_r_naavarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhP.] np. du démon Tṛṇāvarta «Tornade», démon envoyé par <a href="#entry-Uka_msa" class="skt-link">Kaṃsa</a> pour tuer <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> bébé; celui-ci devint aussi lourd qu'un rocher, et l'étouffa.<br> <a href="#entry-t_r_na" class="skt-link">tṛṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_nabindu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tṛṇabindu</i> [<a href="#entry-bindu" class="skt-link">bindu</a>] <a href="#entry-t_r_nabindu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. d'un ancien sage, le prince Tṛṇabindu «Gouttelette»; il est père d'<a href="#entry-Ui_davi_daa" class="skt-link">Iḍaviḍā</a> et de <a href="#entry-Umaaninii" class="skt-link">Māninī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_naku_ncaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tṛṇakuñcaka</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_ncaka" class="skt-link">kuñcaka</a>] <a href="#entry-t_r_naku_ncaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«qui courbe l'herbe (électrostatique)»] ambre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_nama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tṛṇamaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-t_r_nama_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> ambre; syn. <a href="#entry-t_r_naku_ncaka" class="skt-link">tṛṇakuñcaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_napraaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tṛṇaprāya</i> [<a href="#entry-praaya" class="skt-link">prāya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-t_r_napraaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-t_r_napraaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-t_r_napraayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tṛṇaprāyā</i> insignifiant, ne valant pratiquement rien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_naskanda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tṛṇaskanda</i> [<a href="#entry-skanda" class="skt-link">skanda</a>] <a href="#entry-t_r_naskanda" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. sauterelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_s_naa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृष्णा</span> <i>tṛṣṇā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-t_r_s_1" class="skt-link">tṛṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-t_r_s_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soif; désir, convoitise; concupiscence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. le désir, 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ cause de souffrance [<a href="#entry-pratiityasamutpaada" class="skt-link">pratītyasamutpāda</a>] — ifc. avidité de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_s_naka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृष्णक</span> <i>tṛṣṇaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-t_r_s_1" class="skt-link">tṛṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-t_r_s_naka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-t_r_s_naka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-t_r_s_nikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tṛṣṇikā</i> qui désire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_s_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृष्</span> <i>tṛṣ_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(tṛṣyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tṛṣita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> avoir soif; désirer, convoiter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. τερσω; lat. <i>torreo</i>; all. dürsten; ang. thirst; fr. torride.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_s_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृष्</span> <i>tṛṣ_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-t_r_s_1" class="skt-link">tṛṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-t_r_s_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-t_r_s_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-t_r_s_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> ifc. qui convoite ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-t_r_s_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir ardent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृषा</span> <i>tṛṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-t_r_s_1" class="skt-link">tṛṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-t_r_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soif; désir ardent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_r_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृषित</span> <i>tṛṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-t_r_s_1" class="skt-link">tṛṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-t_r_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-t_r_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-t_r_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tṛṣitā</i> altéré, assoiffé; envieux — <a href="#entry-t_r_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> soif, désir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_rc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृच्</span> <i>tṛc</i> nota. gram. affixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] dénotant l'agent du verbe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>tṛn</i> variante indiquant l'habitude de l'action.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_rca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृच</span> <i>tṛca</i> [<a href="#entry-tri" class="skt-link">tri</a>-<a href="#entry-_rc_2" class="skt-link">ṛc_2</a>] <a href="#entry-t_rca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-t_rca" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. strophe consistant de 3 versets.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_rd">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृद्</span> <i>tṛd</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(tṛṇatti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tṛṇṇa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>pra</i><i>)</i> percer; couper, fendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_rp_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृप्</span> <i>tṛp_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(tṛpyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tṛpta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i><i>)</i> être satisfait, content; être rassasié; se plaire à ⟨g. i.⟩; se rassasier, jouir de ⟨abl.⟩ — ca. <i>(tarpayati)</i> satisfaire, contenter, réjouir; réconforter; nourrir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. τρεφω.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_rp_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृप्</span> <i>tṛp_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-t_rp_1" class="skt-link">tṛp_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-t_rp_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-t_rp_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-t_rp_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui jouit de; qui s'approprie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_rpta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृप्त</span> <i>tṛpta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-t_rp_1" class="skt-link">tṛp_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-t_rpta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-t_rpta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-t_rptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tṛptā</i> rassasié, bien nourri; satisfait, comblé, assouvi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> content de ⟨g. i. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_rpti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृप्ति</span> <i>tṛpti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-t_rp_1" class="skt-link">tṛp_1</a>] <a href="#entry-t_rpti" class="skt-link">f.</a> contentement, satiété.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तॄ</span> <i>tṝ</i> var. <i>tṛ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(tarati)</i> opt. <i>(taret)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(tirati)</i> aor. ps. <i>(atāri)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] <i>(atārṣīt)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(atārīt)</i> pft. <i>(tatāra)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tīrṇa</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>tīrtvā</i>, <i>-tīrya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> traverser, franchir, passer à travers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vaincre, surpasser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> atteindre, parvenir à, accomplir, réaliser — ca. <i>(tārayati)</i> faire traverser, conduire; sauver, secourir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> libérer de ⟨abl.⟩ — dés. <i>(titīrṣati)</i> dés. md. <i>(titīrṣate)</i> cf. <a href="#entry-titiir_s" class="skt-link">titīrṣ</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. τερμα; lat. <i>terminus</i>; fr. terminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_rtiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृतीय</span> <i>tṛtīya</i> [<a href="#entry-tri" class="skt-link">tri</a>] a. <a href="#entry-t_rtiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-t_rtiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-t_rtiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tṛtīyā</i> ord. troisième — <a href="#entry-t_rtiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tṛtīyā</i> gram. instrumental (3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ déclinaison) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour d'un demi-mois lunaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. troisième chant de louange [<a href="#entry-stotra" class="skt-link">stotra</a>] chanté lors du sacrifice <a href="#entry-jyoti_s_toma" class="skt-link">jyotiṣṭoma</a> pour le pressurage [<a href="#entry-savana_1" class="skt-link">savana_1</a>] du soir; il est dit syn. <a href="#entry-aarbhava" class="skt-link">ārbhava</a>.<br> <i>tṛtīya</i> <i>nayana</i> myth. le 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ œil de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-t_rtsu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृत्सु</span> <i>tṛtsu</i> <a href="#entry-t_rtsu" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. np. du pays et du peuple Tṛtsu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. ses habitants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तड्</span> <i>taḍ</i> ca. <i>(tāḍayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tāḍita</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>tāḍayitvā</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> taper, frapper, battre; vanner; punir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> taper (dans un ballon); battre (le tambour) — ps. <i>(tāḍyate)</i> être battu, être tapé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_daaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तडाग</span> <i>taḍāga</i> var. <i>taḍāka</i> <a href="#entry-ta_daaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ta_daaga" class="skt-link">n.</a> mare, étang, réservoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_daagaadividhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taḍāgādividhi</i> [<a href="#entry-aadi" class="skt-link">ādi</a>-<a href="#entry-vidhi" class="skt-link">vidhi</a>] <a href="#entry-ta_daagaadividhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> véd. np. de la Taḍāgādividhi, traitant du rituel de consécration des bassins et mares, appendice [<a href="#entry-parizi_s_ta" class="skt-link">pariśiṣṭa</a>] de l'<a href="#entry-Uatharvaveda" class="skt-link">Atharvaveda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ta_daaga" class="skt-link">taḍāga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_dit">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तडित्</span> <i>taḍit</i> [<a href="#entry-ta_d" class="skt-link">taḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-ta_dit" class="skt-link">f.</a> éclair, foudre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_n_du">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तण्डु</span> <i>taṇḍu</i> [<a href="#entry-ta_d" class="skt-link">taḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-ta_n_du" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Taṇḍu «Tambourin», garde de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, réputé inventeur de la mimique et de la danse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_n_dula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तण्डुल</span> <i>taṇḍula</i> [<a href="#entry-ta_d" class="skt-link">taḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-ta_n_dula" class="skt-link">m.</a> riz en grain (vanné); grain de riz <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> unité de poids, not. de gemmes; il vaut 8 grains de moutarde [<a href="#entry-sar_sapa" class="skt-link">sarṣapa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>taṇḍulās</i> riz à cuire.<br> <i>taṇḍulān</i> <i>pacati</i> il cuit du riz.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_n_dulakusumavali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taṇḍulakusumavali</i> [<a href="#entry-kusuma" class="skt-link">kusuma</a>-<a href="#entry-vali" class="skt-link">vali</a>] <a href="#entry-ta_n_dulakusumavali" class="skt-link">f.</a> raies faites avec des fleurs et des grains de riz, not. pour décorer les temples et les seuils de maisons.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_n_dulakusumavalivikaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taṇḍulakusumavalivikāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vikaara" class="skt-link">vikāra</a>] <a href="#entry-ta_n_dulakusumavalivikaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> art de décorer avec des fleurs et des grains de riz, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-ta_n_dulakusumavali" class="skt-link">taṇḍulakusumavali</a> <br> <a href="#entry-ta_n_dula" class="skt-link">taṇḍula</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तष्ट</span> <i>taṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tak_s" class="skt-link">takṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ta_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ta_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ta_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taṣṭā</i> taillé, découpé, façonné; sculpté.<br> <i>ḥṛdā</i> <i>taṣṭān</i> <i>mantrān</i> Les mantras proviennent du cœur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तट</span> <i>taṭa</i> <a href="#entry-ta_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> rive, rivage, berge; déclivité, pente, talus <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bord, côté (not. du corps) — <a href="#entry-ta_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taṭī</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_tastha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taṭastha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ta_tastha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ta_tastha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ta_tasthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taṭasthā</i> qui se tient sur la berge ou le rivage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> personne indifférente.<br> <a href="#entry-ta_ta" class="skt-link">taṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taa_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताड्</span> <i>tāḍ</i> cf. <a href="#entry-ta_d" class="skt-link">taḍ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taa_dana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताडन</span> <i>tāḍana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ta_d" class="skt-link">taḍ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taa_dana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taa_dana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taa_danaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāḍanā</i> qui frappe, qui bat, qui blesse — <a href="#entry-taa_dana" class="skt-link">n.</a> action de battre, de frapper, de fouetter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. éclipse partielle — ifc. (après instrument) fait d'infliger une correction avec — <a href="#entry-taa_danii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāḍanī</i> fouet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taa_dita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताडित</span> <i>tāḍita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-ta_d" class="skt-link">taḍ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taa_dita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taa_dita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taa_ditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāḍitā</i> battu, frappé, puni <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> frappé par la foudre, foudroyé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taa_ditaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāḍitaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-taa_ditaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taa_ditaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taa_ditakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāḍitakā</i> frappé par.<br> <a href="#entry-taa_dita" class="skt-link">tāḍita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taa_dyamaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताड्यमान</span> <i>tāḍyamāna</i> [ppr. ps. <a href="#entry-ta_d" class="skt-link">taḍ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taa_dyamaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taa_dyamaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taa_dyamaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāḍyamānā</i> étant frappé, étant battu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taa_n_dava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताण्डव</span> <i>tāṇḍava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ta_n_du" class="skt-link">taṇḍu</a>] <a href="#entry-taa_n_dava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taa_n_dava" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. danse macabre de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a> dans les enclos crématoires <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>ānandatāṇḍava</i>] myth. danse cosmique de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Una_taraaja" class="skt-link">Naṭarāja</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-nadaanta" class="skt-link">nadānta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taa_n_dya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताण्ड्य</span> <i>tāṇḍya</i> <a href="#entry-taa_n_dya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. patr. du lettré Tāṇḍya — <a href="#entry-taa_n_dya" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-taa_n_dyabraahma_na" class="skt-link">tāṇḍyabrāhmaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taa_n_dyabraahma_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāṇḍyabrāhmaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-taa_n_dyabraahma_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Tāṇḍyabrāhmaṇa, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Umahaabraahma_na" class="skt-link">Mahābrāhmaṇa</a> ou <a href="#entry-Upa_ncavi_mzabraahma_na" class="skt-link">Pañcaviṃśabrāhmaṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-taa_n_dya" class="skt-link">tāṇḍya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taa_tafka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताटङ्क</span> <i>tāṭaṅka</i> <a href="#entry-taa_tafka" class="skt-link">n.</a> ornement d'oreille, symbole femelle; il orne not. l'oreille gauche de <a href="#entry-Una_taraaja" class="skt-link">Naṭarāja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taa_takaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताटका</span> <i>tāṭakā</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ta_ta" class="skt-link">taṭa</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-taa_takaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de la <a href="#entry-yak_sii" class="skt-link">yakṣī</a> Tāṭakā «(Gardienne) de la rive», fille du <a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a> <a href="#entry-Usuketu" class="skt-link">Suketu</a>, «forte comme 1000 éléphants»; elle épousa le <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> <a href="#entry-Usunda_1" class="skt-link">Sunda_1</a>, et enfanta de <a href="#entry-Umaariica" class="skt-link">Mārīca</a> et <a href="#entry-Usubaahu" class="skt-link">Subāhu</a>; après qu'<a href="#entry-Uagastya" class="skt-link">Agastya</a> ait réduit <a href="#entry-Usunda_1" class="skt-link">Sunda_1</a> en cendres pour avoir perturbé ses dévotions, elle attaqua son ermitage avec ses fils, mais <a href="#entry-Uagastya" class="skt-link">Agastya</a> les maudit, et elle devint une hideuse <a href="#entry-raak_sasii" class="skt-link">rākṣasī</a>, hantant une forêt au sud de la <a href="#entry-Usarayuu" class="skt-link">Sarayū</a>; le jeune <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> la tua, sur l'ordre de son <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taacchiilika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताच्छीलिक</span> <i>tācchīlika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tacchiila" class="skt-link">tacchīla</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taacchiilika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taacchiilika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taacchiilikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tācchīlikā</i> gram. se dit d'un suffixe dénotant l'habitude ou le naturel d'une action.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taacchiilya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताच्छील्य</span> <i>tācchīlya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tacchiila" class="skt-link">tacchīla</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-taacchiilya" class="skt-link">n.</a> nature caractéristique; habitude, intérêt pour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taad_rz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तादृश्</span> <i>tādṛś</i> [<a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>-<a href="#entry-d_rz_2" class="skt-link">dṛś_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taad_rz" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taad_rz" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taad_rz" class="skt-link">f.</a> [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yaad_rz" class="skt-link">yādṛś</a>] de cette sorte là, semblable; pareil, tel — a. <a href="#entry-taad_rza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taad_rza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>tādṛśa</i> <a href="#entry-taad_rzii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tādṛśī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taadaatmya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तादात्म्य</span> <i>tādātmya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tadaatma" class="skt-link">tadātma</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-taadaatmya" class="skt-link">n.</a> similitude ou identité de nature avec ⟨i. loc. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taadarthya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तादर्थ्य</span> <i>tādarthya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tadartha" class="skt-link">tadartha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-taadarthya" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'être approprié; utilité; pertinence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. similitude de sens, synonymie.<br> <i>tādarthyena</i> avec cette intention; à cette fin; dans ce sens.<br> <i>tādarthye</i> <i>caturthī</i> <i>vācyā</i> gram. On indique l'utilité avec le datif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taajika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताजिक</span> <i>tājika</i> <a href="#entry-taajika" class="skt-link">m.</a> tadjik.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताल</span> <i>tāla</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tala" class="skt-link">tala</a>] <a href="#entry-taala" class="skt-link">m.</a> verrou; serrure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Borassus flabelliformis</i>, rônier ou palmyre, espèce de palmier à vin au tronc droit; on boit son suc sucré; ses dattes sont comestibles, et il fournit un chou-palmiste; on utilise ses feuilles pour l'écriture; myth. il est l'emblème de <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a>-<a href="#entry-Utaalaketu" class="skt-link">Tālaketu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de frapper dans ses mains, de battre la mesure; cymbale; mouvement rythmique (not. des oreilles d'un éléphant) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mus. rythme (donné par la frappe des cordes d'un instrument avec la paume); cycle rythmique; en musique carnatique, il y a 7 rythmes de base, donnés avec leurs constituants [<a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>]: <a href="#entry-dhruva" class="skt-link">dhruva</a> (1 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a>, 1 <a href="#entry-druta" class="skt-link">druta</a> et 2 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a>), <a href="#entry-matya" class="skt-link">matya</a> (1 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a>, 1 <a href="#entry-druta" class="skt-link">druta</a> et 1 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a>), <a href="#entry-ruupaka" class="skt-link">rūpaka</a> (1 <a href="#entry-druta" class="skt-link">druta</a> et 1 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a>), <a href="#entry-jhampa" class="skt-link">jhampa</a> (1 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a>, 1 <a href="#entry-anudruta" class="skt-link">anudruta</a>, et 1 <a href="#entry-druta" class="skt-link">druta</a>), <a href="#entry-tripu_ta" class="skt-link">tripuṭa</a> (1 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a> et 2 <a href="#entry-druta" class="skt-link">druta</a>), <a href="#entry-a_t_ta" class="skt-link">aṭṭa</a> (2 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a> et 2 <a href="#entry-druta" class="skt-link">druta</a>) et <a href="#entry-eka" class="skt-link">eka</a> (1 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mesure de hauteur, vaut 12 pouces [<a href="#entry-afgula" class="skt-link">aṅgula</a>] (environ 23 cm); syn. <a href="#entry-vitasti" class="skt-link">vitasti</a> — a. <a href="#entry-taala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tālī</i> issu du palmier; fait de bois de palmier — <a href="#entry-taalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tālī</i> natu. bot. <i>Corypha umbraculifera</i>, palmier talipot, natif du Malabar, à grandes feuilles en ombrelles; il peut atteindre 25 m de hauteur; ses feuilles sont utilisées pour l'écriture et pour couvrir les toits, sa sève est fermentée pour faire du vin de palme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> alcool de palmier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. toddy.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taaladhvaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāladhvaja</i> [<a href="#entry-dhvaja" class="skt-link">dhvaja</a>] <a href="#entry-taaladhvaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Tāladhvaja, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a> «portant le palmier sur son étendard» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. np. du char à 16 roues de <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a> au festival du <a href="#entry-rathayaatraa" class="skt-link">rathayātrā</a> de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taalajafgha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tālajaṅgha</i> [<a href="#entry-jafghaa" class="skt-link">jaṅghā</a>] <a href="#entry-taalajafgha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du vaillant roi Tālajaṅgha «Jambe de palmier», fils de <a href="#entry-Ujayadhvaja" class="skt-link">Jayadhvaja</a>; il fut vaincu par <a href="#entry-Uparazuraama" class="skt-link">Paraśurāma</a>; l'aîné <a href="#entry-Uviitihotra" class="skt-link">Vītihotra</a> de ses 100 fils se réfugia dans l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. myth. np. de la tribu <a href="#entry-haihaya" class="skt-link">haihaya</a> des Tālajaṅghās, ses descendants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taalaketu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tālaketu</i> [<a href="#entry-ketu" class="skt-link">ketu</a>] <a href="#entry-taalaketu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Tālaketu, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a> «portant le palmier sur sa bannière».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taalamaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tālamāna</i> [<a href="#entry-maana_2" class="skt-link">māna_2</a>] <a href="#entry-taalamaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> iconométrie; règles canoniques régissant les mesures iconographiques dans le <a href="#entry-zilpazaastra" class="skt-link">śilpaśāstra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-taala" class="skt-link">tāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taalavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तालव्य</span> <i>tālavya</i> [<a href="#entry-taalu" class="skt-link">tālu</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taalavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taalavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taalavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tālavyā</i> phon. palatal — <a href="#entry-taalavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. phonème palatal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taalu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तालु</span> <i>tālu</i> <a href="#entry-taalu" class="skt-link">n.</a> palais (de la bouche).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताम्</span> <i>tām</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-saa_2" class="skt-link">sā_2</a>] ind. elle, cette.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taamasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तामस</span> <i>tāmasa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taamasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taamasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taamasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāmasā</i> [«dont le <a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a> prépondérant est <a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a>»] ténébreux, sombre; ignorant, endormi; passif, inerte — <a href="#entry-taamasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Tāmasa «issu des ténèbres», 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a> du <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taamasika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तामसिक</span> <i>tāmasika</i> [<a href="#entry-taamasa" class="skt-link">tāmasa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taamasika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taamasika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taamasikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāmasikā</i> relatif au <a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a>, de la nature du <a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a>; inerte, passif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taambala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताम्बल</span> <i>tāmbala</i> a. <a href="#entry-taambala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taambala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taambalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāmbalī</i> en chanvre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taambuula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताम्बूल</span> <i>tāmbūla</i> <a href="#entry-taambuula" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Piper betle</i> ou liane bétel, sorte de poivrier — <a href="#entry-taambuula" class="skt-link">n.</a> feuille de bétel, not. à mastiquer avec de la noix d'arec [<a href="#entry-puuga" class="skt-link">pūga</a>] — <a href="#entry-taambuulii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāmbūlī</i> bétel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taambuulabhoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāmbūlabhoga</i> [<a href="#entry-bhoga_2" class="skt-link">bhoga_2</a>] <a href="#entry-taambuulabhoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> plaisir du bétel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taambuulakarafka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāmbūlakaraṅka</i> [<a href="#entry-karafka" class="skt-link">karaṅka</a>] <a href="#entry-taambuulakarafka" class="skt-link">m.</a> nécessaire à bétel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taambuulakarafkavaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāmbūlakaraṅkavāha</i> [<a href="#entry-vaaha" class="skt-link">vāha</a>] <a href="#entry-taambuulakarafkavaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> serviteur d'un prince chargé de porter son bétel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taambuulakarafkavaahinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāmbūlakaraṅkavāhinī</i> [<a href="#entry-vaahinii" class="skt-link">vāhinī</a>] <a href="#entry-taambuulakarafkavaahinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> suivante d'un prince chargée de porter son bétel.<br> <a href="#entry-taambuulakarafka" class="skt-link">tāmbūlakaraṅka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taambuulika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāmbūlika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-taambuulika" class="skt-link">m.</a> vendeur de bétel.<br> <a href="#entry-taambuula" class="skt-link">tāmbūla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taamra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताम्र</span> <i>tāmra</i> a. <a href="#entry-taamra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taamra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taamraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāmrā</i> cuivré, rouge foncé — <a href="#entry-taamraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāmrā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Tāmrā «la Rouge», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>; par <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> elle engendra cinq filles qui engendrèrent à leur tour les cinq races d'oiseaux — <a href="#entry-taamrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāmrī</i> en cuivre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taamradviipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāmradvīpa</i> [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] <a href="#entry-taamradviipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Tāmradvīpa, Ceylan l'«Île couleur de cuivre»; connue des grecs comme Taprobane, des arabes comme Serendib, des portugais comme Ceilão, des anglais comme Ceylon; syn. <a href="#entry-Usvar_nadviipa" class="skt-link">Svarṇadvīpa</a>, <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a> <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>; myth. <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> y établit sa capitale <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>; bd. c'est près de ses côtes que le bateau de <a href="#entry-Usi_mhala" class="skt-link">Siṃhala</a> coula avec ses 700 compagnons <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>Tambapanni</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taamrapa_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāmrapaṭṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_t_ta" class="skt-link">paṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-taamrapa_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-taamrapattra" class="skt-link">tāmrapattra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taamrapar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāmraparṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-par_na" class="skt-link">parṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-taamrapar_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. l'une des 10 régions [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] formant le <a href="#entry-jambudviipa" class="skt-link">jambudvīpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taamrapattra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāmrapattra</i> [<a href="#entry-pattra" class="skt-link">pattra</a>] <a href="#entry-taamrapattra" class="skt-link">n.</a> feuille de cuivre pour écrire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. acte de propriété de terres inscrit sur une feuille de cuivre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taamraprazasti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāmrapraśasti</i> [<a href="#entry-prazasti" class="skt-link">praśasti</a>] <a href="#entry-taamraprazasti" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. plaque de cuivre actant une donation royale.<br> <a href="#entry-taamra" class="skt-link">tāmra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तान</span> <i>tāna</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tan_1" class="skt-link">tan_1</a>] <a href="#entry-taana" class="skt-link">m.</a> ton, résonance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. τονος; fr. ton.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taani">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तानि</span> <i>tāni</i> [nom. pl. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] ind. eux, ces, ils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taantrika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तान्त्रिक</span> <i>tāntrika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taantrika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taantrika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taantrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāntrikā</i> enseigné dans un manuel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. tantrique — <a href="#entry-taantrika" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. adepte des doctrines tantriques.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taantrikaabhidhaanakoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāntrikābhidhānakośa</i> [<a href="#entry-abhidhaanakoza" class="skt-link">abhidhānakośa</a>] <a href="#entry-taantrikaabhidhaanakoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tāntrikābhidhānakośa «Vocabulaire du tantrisme».<br> <a href="#entry-taantrika" class="skt-link">tāntrika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताप्</span> <i>tāp</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(tāpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tāpita</i><i>)</i> faire souffrir, brûler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताप</span> <i>tāpa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] <a href="#entry-taapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> tourment; peine (morale et physique) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. marque au feu du <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a> et de la <a href="#entry-zafkaa" class="skt-link">śaṅkā</a> sur les épaules des renonçants de la secte de <a href="#entry-Uraamaanuja" class="skt-link">Rāmānuja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taapaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तापक</span> <i>tāpaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taapaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taapaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taapikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāpikā</i> qui chauffe, qui enflamme; qui tourmente — <a href="#entry-taapaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> fièvre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fourneau — <a href="#entry-taapikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāpikā</i> poêle à frire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taapana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तापन</span> <i>tāpana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taapana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taapana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taapanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāpanī</i> qui brûle, qui fair souffrir — <a href="#entry-taapana" class="skt-link">m.</a> le Soleil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> la canicule — <a href="#entry-taapana" class="skt-link">n.</a> brûlure; tourment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [théâtre] détresse, perplexité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> np. de Tāpana, un enfer brûlant — <a href="#entry-taapanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāpanā</i> ascèse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taapasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तापस</span> <i>tāpasa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a>] <a href="#entry-taapasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> ascète, ermite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taapatraya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāpatraya</i> [<a href="#entry-traya" class="skt-link">traya</a>] <a href="#entry-taapatraya" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. les trois sortes de maux: [<a href="#entry-aadhyaatmika" class="skt-link">ādhyātmika</a>] les tourments internes du corps et de l'esprit (faim, soif, colère, jalousie, confusion), [<a href="#entry-aadhibhautika" class="skt-link">ādhibhautika</a>] les maux séculiers externes (attaques d'animaux ou d'ennemis, guerres) et [<a href="#entry-aadhidaivika" class="skt-link">ādhidaivika</a>] les maux dûs aux calamités naturelles ou surnaturelles (tempêtes, tremblements de terre, épidémies).<br> <a href="#entry-taapa" class="skt-link">tāpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taapatya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तापत्य</span> <i>tāpatya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tapatii" class="skt-link">tapatī</a>] <a href="#entry-taapatya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Tāpatya «descendant de <a href="#entry-Utapatii" class="skt-link">Tapatī</a>», matr. de <a href="#entry-Ukuru" class="skt-link">Kuru</a>; épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taapin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तापिन्</span> <i>tāpin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taapin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taapin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taapinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāpinī</i> qui fait souffrir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> souffrant de la chaleur; souffrant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taapita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तापित</span> <i>tāpita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taapita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taapita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taapitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāpitā</i> chauffé, enflammé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tourmenté, meurtri <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> converti.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तार्</span> <i>tār</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(tārayati)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>tārya</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(tārayitum)</i> faire traverser, conduire; sauver, secourir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> libérer de ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तार</span> <i>tāra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-taar" class="skt-link">tār</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tārā</i> qui fait traverser; sauveur, libérateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> brillant, resplendissant; haut, perçant (son) — <a href="#entry-taara" class="skt-link">m.</a> éclat; son strident — <a href="#entry-taara" class="skt-link">n.</a> argent — <a href="#entry-taaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tārā</i> étoile, astre, météore <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Tārā «Étoile», épouse de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>; elle fut enlevée par <a href="#entry-Usoma" class="skt-link">Soma</a>-<a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a> dont elle eut pour fils <a href="#entry-Ubudha" class="skt-link">Budha</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ucandramasii" class="skt-link">Candramasī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] np. de Tārā_1 «Libératrice», l'une des 10 puissances [<a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>] de la Déesse [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Tārā_2, épouse du roi des singes <a href="#entry-Uvaalii" class="skt-link">Vālī</a>; à sa mort, elle épousa son demi-frère <a href="#entry-Usugriiva" class="skt-link">Sugrīva</a>; elle maudit <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> de perdre <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> après qu'il la retrouve <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. nom de diverses déesses; not. la Tārā blanche et la Tārā verte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. star.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तारण</span> <i>tāraṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taara_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāraṇī</i> qui fait traverser, qui aide à passer; sauveur, libérateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> «Sauveur» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Tāraṇa «Salut», personnifiant l'an 18 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>] — <a href="#entry-taara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de se tirer d'une difficulté; traversée, passage; salut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taaraanaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tārānātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-taaraanaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. np. de Tārānātha «Maître de l'Étoile», titre de lettré [<a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taaraapii_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tārāpīḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-aapii_da" class="skt-link">āpīḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-taaraapii_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. du roi d'Ujjayinī Tārāpīḍa «Couronné d'étoiles», père de <a href="#entry-Ucandraapii_da_1" class="skt-link">Candrāpīḍa_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taaraavalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tārāvalī</i> [<a href="#entry-aavalii" class="skt-link">āvalī</a>] <a href="#entry-taaraavalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [DKC.] np. de la princesse <a href="#entry-yak_sii" class="skt-link">yakṣī</a> Tārāvalī «Lignée d'étoiles», fille du roi <a href="#entry-Uma_nibhadra" class="skt-link">Maṇibhadra</a>; elle recueillit <a href="#entry-Uarthapaala" class="skt-link">Arthapāla</a>, fruit des amours de <a href="#entry-Ukaamapaala" class="skt-link">Kāmapāla</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ukaantimatii" class="skt-link">Kāntimatī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-taara" class="skt-link">tāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taaraga_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tāragaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-taaraga_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> multitude d'étoiles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तारक</span> <i>tāraka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-taar" class="skt-link">tār</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taaraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tārikā</i> qui fait traverser, qui transporte; sauveur, libérateur — <a href="#entry-taaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Tāraka «Astre», <a href="#entry-daitya" class="skt-link">daitya</a> qui par ses austérités acquit de grands pouvoirs et menaça les trois mondes; il obtint de ne pouvoir être tué que par un fils de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; il eut trois fils, <a href="#entry-Utaarakaak_sa" class="skt-link">Tārakākṣa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvidyunmaalii" class="skt-link">Vidyunmālī</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukamalaak_sa" class="skt-link">Kamalākṣa</a> (cf. <a href="#entry-Utripura" class="skt-link">Tripura</a>); il fut vaincu par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> grâce à l'aide de <a href="#entry-Ukumaara" class="skt-link">Kumāra</a>-<a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-taarakaa" class="skt-link">tārakā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taarakaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तारका</span> <i>tārakā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-taaraka" class="skt-link">tāraka</a>] <a href="#entry-taarakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> étoile; syn. <a href="#entry-taaraa" class="skt-link">tārā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Tārakā «Étoile», autre nom de <a href="#entry-Utaaraa" class="skt-link">Tārā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. étoile principale d'un <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-yogataaraa" class="skt-link">yogatārā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taarakaak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tārakākṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sa" class="skt-link">akṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-taarakaak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Tārakākṣa «qui a des étoiles dans les yeux», fils de <a href="#entry-Utaaraka" class="skt-link">Tāraka</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Utripura" class="skt-link">Tripura</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taarakaamaya_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tārakāmaya_1</i> [<a href="#entry-aamaya" class="skt-link">āmaya</a>] <a href="#entry-taarakaamaya_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. destruction du démon <a href="#entry-Utaaraka" class="skt-link">Tāraka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taarakaamaya_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tārakāmaya_2</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taarakaamaya_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taarakaamaya_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taarakaamayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tārakāmayī</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Utaarakaa" class="skt-link">Tārakā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taarakaamayayuddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tārakāmayayuddha</i> [<a href="#entry-yuddha" class="skt-link">yuddha</a>] <a href="#entry-taarakaamayayuddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. [VP.] guerre à propos d'Étoile, provoquée par l'enlèvement de <a href="#entry-Utaaraa" class="skt-link">Tārā</a> par <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-taarakaamaya_2" class="skt-link">tārakāmaya_2</a> <br> <a href="#entry-taarakaa" class="skt-link">tārakā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taarakaaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tārakārāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-taarakaaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Tārakārāja, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a> «Roi des étoiles».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taarakita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tārakita</i> [<a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taarakita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taarakita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taarakitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tārakitā</i> étoilé (se dit du ciel de début de nuit).<br> <a href="#entry-taaraka" class="skt-link">tāraka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taaratamya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तारतम्य</span> <i>tāratamya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-taratama" class="skt-link">taratama</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-taaratamya" class="skt-link">n.</a> gradation, proportion; différence, inégalité.<br> <i>tāratamyena</i> adv. à différents degrés; plus ou moins.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तारिन्</span> <i>tārin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāriṇī</i> qui fait traverser; sauveur, libérateur — <a href="#entry-taari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāriṇī</i> myth. np. de Tāriṇī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> «Libératrice».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taark_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तार्क्ष्य</span> <i>tārkṣya</i> <a href="#entry-taark_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. np. de Tārkṣya, divinité solaire, d'abord cheval (aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uari_s_tanemi" class="skt-link">Ariṣṭanemi</a>), puis oiseau, identifié plus tard avec <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taarkika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तार्किक</span> <i>tārkika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tarka" class="skt-link">tarka</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taarkika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taarkika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taarkikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tārkikā</i> relatif à la logique — <a href="#entry-taarkika" class="skt-link">m.</a> dialecticien; logicien; philosophe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तार्य</span> <i>tārya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-taar" class="skt-link">tār</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāryā</i> à franchir, à traverser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तास्</span> <i>tās</i> [nom. pl. <a href="#entry-saa_2" class="skt-link">sā_2</a>] ind. elles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तात</span> <i>tāta</i> [<a href="#entry-tata_2" class="skt-link">tata_2</a>] <a href="#entry-taata" class="skt-link">m.</a> père — voc. adr. affectueuse envers un parent mâle, junior ou senior <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>tātās</i> chers tous.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ताति</span> <i>tāti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tan_1" class="skt-link">tan_1</a>] <a href="#entry-taati" class="skt-link">f.</a> continuité; descendance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taatparya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तात्पर्य</span> <i>tātparya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tatpara" class="skt-link">tatpara</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taatparya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taatparya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taatparyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tātparyā</i> visé — <a href="#entry-taatparya" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de se dévouer à ça <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> référence à ⟨loc.⟩; intention; but visé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] transmission de l'intention du locuteur — ifc. lit. ouvrage à propos de ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taatparya_tiikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tātparyaṭīkā</i> [<a href="#entry-_tiikaa" class="skt-link">ṭīkā</a>] <a href="#entry-taatparya_tiikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Tātparyaṭīkā, glose par <a href="#entry-Uvaacaspati_1" class="skt-link">Vācaspati_1</a> <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a> du <a href="#entry-Unyaayabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Nyāyabhāṣya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taatparyaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tātparyārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-taatparyaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. sens d'un mot visé par le locuteur.<br> <a href="#entry-taatparya" class="skt-link">tātparya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taatparyacandrikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tātparyacandrikā</i> [<a href="#entry-candrikaa" class="skt-link">candrikā</a>] <a href="#entry-taatparyacandrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Tātparyacandrikā, commentaire [<a href="#entry-_tiikaa" class="skt-link">ṭīkā</a>] de la <a href="#entry-Ugiitaa" class="skt-link">Gītā</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uvedaantadezika" class="skt-link">Vedāntadeśika</a>, suivant <a href="#entry-Uraamaanuja" class="skt-link">Rāmānuja</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la Tātparyacandrikā_1 de <a href="#entry-Uvyaasatiirtha" class="skt-link">Vyāsatīrtha</a>, commentaire du Tattvatātparya de <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a>; il lui valut le titre de <a href="#entry-Ucandrikaacaarya" class="skt-link">Candrikācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taavaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तावक</span> <i>tāvaka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tava" class="skt-link">tava</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taavaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taavaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taavakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāvakī</i> ton; tien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तावत्</span> <i>tāvat</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tāvatī</i> aussi grand, aussi nombreux, aussi longtemps, autant — adv. [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yaavat" class="skt-link">yāvat</a>] autant, tant, comme, pendant aussi longtemps <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> d'abord; seulement; soit! <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ⟨interr. ou imp.⟩ donc <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ⟨fut. ou pr.⟩ alors (indique que l'action va avoir lieu).<br> <i>mā</i> <i>tāvat</i> Dieu nous en garde!.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तच्</span> <i>tac</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tacca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tacca</i> [<a href="#entry-ca" class="skt-link">ca</a>] adv. et ceci.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tacchiila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tacchīla</i> [<a href="#entry-ziila" class="skt-link">śīla</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tacchiila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tacchiila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tacchiilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tacchīlā</i> ayant cette habitude; de cette nature.<br> <a href="#entry-tac" class="skt-link">tac</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तद्</span> <i>tad</i> var. <i>tac</i>, <i>tat</i>, <i>tan</i> pn. 3ème pers. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-sa_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>sa_2</i> <a href="#entry-saa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>sā_2</i> il, ce, cet, cela <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yad" class="skt-link">yad</a>] (celui qui ...) il <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> donc; pour cette raison; alors, en cela, c'est que.<br> <i>tad</i> <i>etad</i> adv. cela même.<br> <i>tat</i> <i>tad</i> ceci et cela; tel ou tel; différents, variés; respectifs <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] cet ⟨ifc.⟩ précisément.<br> <i>taṃ</i> <i>taṃ</i> <i>deśaṃ</i> <i>jagāma</i> il alla ici et là.<br> <i>yat</i> <i>tad</i> quoi que ce soit, n'importe lequel; tout.<br> <i>yad</i> <i>vā</i> <i>tad</i> <i>vā</i> ceci ou cela, n'importe lequel.<br> <i>tad</i> <i>yathā</i> adv. notamment, comme suit.<br> <i>tadapi</i> adv. néanmoins, cependant.<br> <i>tadvat</i> [corrél. <a href="#entry-yadvat" class="skt-link">yadvat</a>] conj. ainsi; comme cela.<br> <i>tat</i> <i>tvam</i> <i>asi</i> phil. [ChU.] «Tu es cela», formule résumant la doctrine <a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तदा</span> <i>tadā</i> [<a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a><a href="#entry--daa" class="skt-link">-dā</a>] adv. [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yadaa" class="skt-link">yadā</a>] alors, dans ce cas.<br> <i>tadātadā</i> [corrél. <a href="#entry-yadaayadaa" class="skt-link">yadāyadā</a>] conj. alors (à chaque fois).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadaaniim">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tadānīm</i> [<a href="#entry--niim" class="skt-link">-nīm</a>] adv. alors, en ce temps là.<br> <i>nāsad</i> <i>āsīn</i> <i>no</i> <i>sad</i> <i>āsīt</i> <i>tadānīm</i> [RV.] (Nāsādisūkta, Hymne de création) En ce temps là, ni le néant n'existait, ni l'être n'existait.<br> <a href="#entry-tadaa" class="skt-link">tadā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadaatma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tadātma</i> [<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tadaatma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tadaatma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tadaatmanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tadātmanī</i> de cet esprit, de cette essence; de cette nature.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadanantara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tadanantara</i> [<a href="#entry-anantara" class="skt-link">anantara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tadanantara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tadanantara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tadanantaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tadanantarā</i> tout proche de ⟨g.⟩; adjacent, contigu; qui succède immédiatement à.<br> <i>tadanantaram</i> adv. juste après, à la suite de quoi, là-dessus, c'est alors que, sur ces entrefaites.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadanya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tadanya</i> [<a href="#entry-anya" class="skt-link">anya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tadanya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tadanya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tadanyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tadanyā</i> autre que ça.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tadartha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tadartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tadartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tadarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tadarthā</i> à cet usage; propre à ça, qui sert à ça; intentionnel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. dans ce sens; avec le même sens — <a href="#entry-tadartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> cet usage; ce sens.<br> <i>tadartham</i> adv. pour cette raison; en vue de cet objet; en conséquence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadbhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tadbhava</i> [<a href="#entry-bhava" class="skt-link">bhava</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tadbhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tadbhava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tadbhavaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tadbhavā</i> dérivé (du sanskrit) — <a href="#entry-tadbhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. mot d'origine indo-aryenne; cf. <a href="#entry-tatsama" class="skt-link">tatsama</a>; opp. <a href="#entry-apabhra_mza" class="skt-link">apabhraṃśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taddharma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taddharma</i> [<a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taddharma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taddharma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taddharmaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taddharmā</i> dont c'est la tâche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taddhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taddhita</i> [<a href="#entry-hita_2" class="skt-link">hita_2</a>] <a href="#entry-taddhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. suffixe [<a href="#entry-pratyaya" class="skt-link">pratyaya</a>] secondaire (forme des substantifs à partir de substantifs); opp. <a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taddhitaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taddhitānta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] <a href="#entry-taddhitaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. dérivé secondaire.<br> <a href="#entry-taddhita" class="skt-link">taddhita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadruupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tadrūpa</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tadruupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tadruupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tadruupaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tadrūpā</i> de même forme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तद्वत्</span> <i>tadvat</i> [<a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tadvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tadvat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tadvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tadvatī</i> de cette forme; semblable à ça — ind. ainsi, donc, comme (corr. de <a href="#entry-yadvat" class="skt-link">yadvat</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pareillement, aussi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadvid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tadvid</i> [<a href="#entry-vid_3" class="skt-link">vid_3</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tadvid" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tadvid" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tadvid" class="skt-link">f.</a> compétent, spécialiste, expert.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadvidya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tadvidya</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tadvidya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tadvidya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tadvidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tadvidyā</i> connaisseur, expert.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tadvidyasambhaa_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tadvidyasaṃbhāṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-sambhaa_saa" class="skt-link">saṃbhāṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-tadvidyasa_mbhaa_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> entretien avec un spécialiste.<br> <a href="#entry-tadvidya" class="skt-link">tadvidya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taijasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तैजस</span> <i>taijasa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tejas" class="skt-link">tejas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taijasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taijasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taijasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taijasā</i> lumineux, brillant; à l'éclat métallique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तैल</span> <i>taila</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tila" class="skt-link">tila</a>] <a href="#entry-taila" class="skt-link">n.</a> huile (not. de sésame ou de margousier).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tailama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tailamaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-tailama_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> ambre.<br> <a href="#entry-taila" class="skt-link">taila</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tailapii_daa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tailapīḍā</i> [<a href="#entry-pii_daa" class="skt-link">pīḍā</a>] <a href="#entry-tailapii_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pressoir à huile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tailapuura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tailapūra</i> [<a href="#entry-puura" class="skt-link">pūra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tailapuura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tailapuura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tailapuuraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tailapūrā</i> rempli d'huile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tailatila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tailatila</i> [<a href="#entry-tila" class="skt-link">tila</a>] <a href="#entry-tailatila" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] «graine possédant l'huile», métaphore de l'esprit [<a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">jīva</a>-<a href="#entry-pradhaana" class="skt-link">pradhāna</a>] imprégné de la conscience spirituelle [<a href="#entry-brahmatattva" class="skt-link">brahmatattva</a>-<a href="#entry-k_setraj_na" class="skt-link">kṣetrajña</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taimirika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तैमिरिक</span> <i>taimirika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-timira" class="skt-link">timira</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taimirika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taimirika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taimirikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taimirikā</i> qui a la vision abscurcie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taimirya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तैमिर्य</span> <i>taimirya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-timira" class="skt-link">timira</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-taimirya" class="skt-link">n.</a> problème de vision.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tais">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तैस्</span> <i>tais</i> [i. pl. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] ind. par ces.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taitila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तैतिल</span> <i>taitila</i> <a href="#entry-taitila" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. quatrième demi-jour lunaire [<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] (mobile).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taittira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तैत्तिर</span> <i>taittira</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tittira" class="skt-link">tittira</a>] a. <a href="#entry-taittira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taittira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taittirii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taittirī</i> relatif à la perdrix <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. issu du sage <a href="#entry-Utittiri" class="skt-link">Tittiri</a> — <a href="#entry-taittira" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. perdrix.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taittiriiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taittirīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-taittiriiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. nom d'une école du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> noir [«créée par <a href="#entry-Utittiri" class="skt-link">Tittiri</a>»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. nom de la Taittirīya <a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>, principal recueil du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> noir; on dit que le <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> fut enseigné par <a href="#entry-Uvaizampaayana" class="skt-link">Vaiśampāyana</a> à 27 élèves, dont <a href="#entry-Uyaaj_navalkya" class="skt-link">Yājñavalkya</a>; s'estimant offensé par ce dernier, il lui ordonna de vomir le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> qu'il lui avait appris, et à ses autres disciples, transformés en perdrix, de régurgiter ce texte «noirci»; <a href="#entry-Uyaaj_navalkya" class="skt-link">Yājñavalkya</a> reçut alors du Soleil la version «blanchie», ainsi dite <a href="#entry-vaajasaneyin" class="skt-link">vājasaneyin</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>taittirīyās</i> les adeptes de cette école.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taittiriiyaara_nyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taittirīyāraṇyaka</i> [<a href="#entry-aara_nyaka" class="skt-link">āraṇyaka</a>] <a href="#entry-taittiriiyaara_nyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Taittirīyāraṇyaka, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Umahaanaaraaya_nopani_sad" class="skt-link">Mahānārāyaṇopaniṣad</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taittiriiyapraatizaakhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taittirīyaprātiśākhya</i> [<a href="#entry-praatizaakhya" class="skt-link">prātiśākhya</a>] <a href="#entry-taittiriiyapraatizaakhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Taittirīyaprātiśākhya, recueil phonético-grammatical du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> noir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taittiriiyasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taittirīyasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-taittiriiyasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Taittirīysaṃhitā du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> noir [<a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_nayajurveda" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇayajurveda</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taittiriiyopani_sad">
