  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taittirīyopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-taittiriiyopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la leçon Taittirīyopaniṣad.<br> <a href="#entry-taittiriiya" class="skt-link">taittirīya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-taittira" class="skt-link">taittira</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tak">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तक्</span> <i>tak</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(takti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>takta</i><i>)</i> se précipiter — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(tanakti)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tak_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तक्ष्</span> <i>takṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(takṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>taṣṭa</i><i>)</i> tailler (à la hache), découper, fendre; façonner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तक्ष</span> <i>takṣa</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-tak_san" class="skt-link">takṣan</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tak_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तक्षण</span> <i>takṣaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tak_sa" class="skt-link">takṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-tak_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> qui taille, qui découpe; ciseau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. diviseur — <a href="#entry-tak_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> découpage, charpenterie, façonnage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tak_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तक्षक</span> <i>takṣaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tak_s" class="skt-link">takṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-tak_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> charpentier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Takṣaka «Pourfendeur», roi dragon [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>] allié de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du cobra entourant le bras de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tak_san">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तक्षन्</span> <i>takṣan</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tak_s" class="skt-link">takṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-tak_san" class="skt-link">m.</a> bûcheron, charpentier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Takṣā «Charpentier», fils aîné de <a href="#entry-Ubharata_1" class="skt-link">Bharata_1</a> — iic. cf. <a href="#entry-tak_sa" class="skt-link">takṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tak_sazilaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>takṣaśilā</i> [<a href="#entry-zilaa" class="skt-link">śilā</a>] <a href="#entry-tak_sazilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de Takṣaśilā «Ville des tailleurs de pierre», capitale du <a href="#entry-Ugandhaara" class="skt-link">Gandhāra</a> oriental, centre d'enseignement médical et bouddhique jusqu'au 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; l'empereur <a href="#entry-Uazoka" class="skt-link">Aśoka</a> y fit ses études; son nom fut corrompu en Takṣilā ou Taxila par les Grecs et devint Takkasilā en <a href="#entry-paali" class="skt-link">pāli</a>; la ville fut détruite par les Huns <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>takṣilā</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-tak_sa" class="skt-link">takṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-takaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तकार</span> <i>takāra</i> [<i>(ta)</i>-<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-takaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> le son ou la lettre ‘<i>ta</i>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-takarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तकरी</span> <i>takarī</i> <a href="#entry-takarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> clitoris.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-takra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तक्र</span> <i>takra</i> [<a href="#entry-ta_nc" class="skt-link">tañc</a>] <a href="#entry-takra" class="skt-link">n.</a> babeurre, petit lait.<br> <i>takrakauṇḍinyanyāya</i> gram. maxime de l'exception, par analogie aux brâhmanes <a href="#entry-kau_n_dinya" class="skt-link">kauṇḍinya</a>, qui ont droit au petit-lait plutôt qu'à la crème.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तल</span> <i>tala</i> <a href="#entry-tala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tala" class="skt-link">n.</a> surface, sol <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> paume de la main; poignée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-talaatala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>talātala</i> [<a href="#entry-atala" class="skt-link">atala</a>] <a href="#entry-talaatala" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. du monde [<a href="#entry-loka" class="skt-link">loka</a>] Talātala, cinquième enfer souterrain.<br> <a href="#entry-talava" class="skt-link">talava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-talatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>talatra</i> [<a href="#entry--tra" class="skt-link">-tra</a>] <a href="#entry-talatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> gant porté par les archers.<br> <a href="#entry-tala" class="skt-link">tala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-talava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तलव</span> <i>talava</i> <a href="#entry-talava" class="skt-link">m.</a> musicien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-talavakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>talavakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-talavakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. école du <a href="#entry-Usaamaveda" class="skt-link">Sāmaveda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-talavakaarabraahma_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>talavakārabrāhmaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-talavakaarabraahma_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Talavakārabrāhmaṇa, texte <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> du <a href="#entry-Usaamaveda" class="skt-link">Sāmaveda</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-Ujaiminiiya" class="skt-link">Jaiminīya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-talavakaara" class="skt-link">talavakāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-talpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तल्प</span> <i>talpa</i> [<a href="#entry-t_rp_1" class="skt-link">tṛp_1</a>] <a href="#entry-talpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit, couche, banquette <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> étage supérieur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> radeau — <a href="#entry-talpaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>talpā</i> couche, lit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-talpaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>talpaga</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-talpaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> qui a des rapports sexuels avec ⟨iic.⟩.<br> <a href="#entry-talpa" class="skt-link">talpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तम्</span> <i>tam</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(tāmyati)</i> pft. <i>(tatāma)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tamita</i><i>)</i> être affligé, languir, s'attrister <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> défaillir, suffoquer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tamaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तमाल</span> <i>tamāla</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a>] <a href="#entry-tamaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Xanthochymus pictorius</i> (var. bot. <i>Garcinia xanthochymus</i>), laurier noir ou faux mangoustan, arbre de moyenne altitude, à l'écorce très sombre, aux fleurs blanches, aux fruits orange comestibles; sa sève sert à fabriquer une encre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tamaalaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तमालक</span> <i>tamālaka</i> [<a href="#entry-tamaala" class="skt-link">tamāla</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tamaalaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tamaalaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tamaalikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tamālikā</i> à l'écorce ou à la peau noire — <a href="#entry-tamaalaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. laurier noir — <a href="#entry-tamaalikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tamālikā</i> lit. np. de Tamālikā «au teint d'ébène», servante-messagère de <a href="#entry-Uvaasavadattaa_1" class="skt-link">Vāsavadattā_1</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. de Tamālikā_1, porteuse de bétel de <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tamaalapattra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tamālapattra</i> [<a href="#entry-pattra" class="skt-link">pattra</a>] <a href="#entry-tamaalapattra" class="skt-link">n.</a> feuille de laurier noir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. parque sectaire sur le front.<br> <a href="#entry-tamaala" class="skt-link">tamāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tamas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तमस्</span> <i>tamas</i> [<a href="#entry-tam" class="skt-link">tam</a><a href="#entry--as" class="skt-link">-as</a>] <a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">n.</a> obscurité, ténèbres, noirceur; torpeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>tamoguṇa</i>] phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] la Ténèbre, 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ qualité [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] de la nature, essence passive de l'ignorance et de l'inertie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>temere</i>; all. Dämmerung; ang. dim; fr. ténèbres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tamasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तमस</span> <i>tamasa</i> [<a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tamasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tamasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tamasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tamasā</i> sombre, ténébreux — <a href="#entry-tamasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> obscurité — <a href="#entry-tamasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> ifc. noirceur de — <a href="#entry-tamasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tamasā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Tamasā, tributaire de la <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a> au sud de la <a href="#entry-Uyamunaa" class="skt-link">Yamunā</a>; myth. c'est sur ses bords que <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> passa sa première nuit d'exil, accompagné de nombreux habitants d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a>; <a href="#entry-Uvaalmiiki" class="skt-link">Vālmīki</a> avait son ermitage sur ses bords; c'est là que <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> se réfugia avec ses deux fils lorsqu'elle fut répudiée par <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tamasvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tamasvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tamasvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tamasvat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tamasvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tamasvatī</i> sombre, obscur — <a href="#entry-tamasvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tamasvatī</i> nuit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tamasvin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tamasvin</i> [<a href="#entry--vin" class="skt-link">-vin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tamasvin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tamasvin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tamasvinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tamasvinī</i> rempli d'obscurité — <a href="#entry-tamasvinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tamasvinī</i> nuit.<br> <i>tamasvinyām</i> dans la nuit.<br> <a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tamisra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तमिस्र</span> <i>tamisra</i> [<a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-tamisra" class="skt-link">n.</a> obscurité; nuit noire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>tamisrāṇi</i> id. — <a href="#entry-tamisraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tamisrā</i> nuit noire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tamo">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तमो</span> <i>tamo</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tamogu_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tamoguṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-tamogu_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] la Ténèbre, 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ qualité [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] de la nature, essence passive de l'ignorance et de l'inertie.<br> <a href="#entry-tamo" class="skt-link">tamo</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्</span> <i>tan</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tan_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्</span> <i>tan_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(tanoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(tanute)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>taniṣyati</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tata</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> tendre, étendre; allonger <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> exécuter, accomplir (le sacrifice) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>tendo</i>; fr. tendre.<br> <i>lakṣmīṃ</i> <i>tan</i> ajouter au lustre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tan_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्</span> <i>tan_2</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-stan" class="skt-link">stan</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(tanyati)</i> véd. tonner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. τονος; fr. tonnerre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन</span> <i>tana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tan_1" class="skt-link">tan_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tana" class="skt-link">m.</a> descendance, progéniture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तनय</span> <i>tanaya</i> [<a href="#entry-tana" class="skt-link">tana</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-tanaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui continue la race»] fils — <a href="#entry-tanayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tanayā</i> fille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanayitnu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तनयित्नु</span> <i>tanayitnu</i> var. <i>stanayitnu</i> [<a href="#entry-tan_2" class="skt-link">tan_2</a><a href="#entry--yitnu" class="skt-link">-yitnu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tanayitnu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tanayitnu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tanayitnu" class="skt-link">f.</a> tonnant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tandr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्द्र्</span> <i>tandr</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. md. <i>(tandrayate)</i> se fatiguer (de ⟨inf.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tandraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्द्रा</span> <i>tandrā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tandr" class="skt-link">tandr</a>] <a href="#entry-tandraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lassitude, fatigue; épuisement; paresse — <a href="#entry-tandri" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>tandri</i> ifc. fatigue de — <a href="#entry-tandrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tandrī</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tandraalu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tandrālu</i> [<a href="#entry--lu" class="skt-link">-lu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tandraalu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tandraalu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tandraalu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui tombe de fatigue.<br> <a href="#entry-tandraa" class="skt-link">tandrā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tandrita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्द्रित</span> <i>tandrita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tandr" class="skt-link">tandr</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tandrita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tandrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tandritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tandritā</i> las, fatigué — <a href="#entry-tandritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tandritā</i> lassitude, besoin de sommeil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanmaatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanmātra</i> [<a href="#entry-maatra" class="skt-link">mātra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tanmaatraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tanmātrā</i> juste autant — <a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> juste un peu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] substance [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] de perception, l'un des 5 éléments subtils perçus resp. par les 5 sens [<a href="#entry-buddhiindriya" class="skt-link">buddhīndriya</a>]: <a href="#entry-zabda" class="skt-link">śabda</a> le son (cf. <a href="#entry-zrotra" class="skt-link">śrotra</a>), <a href="#entry-sparza" class="skt-link">sparśa</a> le toucher (cf. <a href="#entry-tvak" class="skt-link">tvak</a>), <a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a> l'aspect ou forme (cf. <a href="#entry-cak_sus" class="skt-link">cakṣus</a>, <a href="#entry-ak_si" class="skt-link">akṣi</a>), <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a> la saveur (cf. <a href="#entry-jihvaa" class="skt-link">jihvā</a>) et <a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a> l'odeur (cf. <a href="#entry-ghraa_na" class="skt-link">ghrāṇa</a>); syn. <a href="#entry-suuk_smabhuuta" class="skt-link">sūkṣmabhūta</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-divyavi_saya" class="skt-link">divyaviṣaya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tan" class="skt-link">tan</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanmadhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanmadhya</i> [<a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">madhya</a>] <a href="#entry-tanmadhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> au milieu de cela.<br> <i>tanmadhyāt</i> parmi eux; hors de leurs rangs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanmanas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanmanas</i> [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tanmanas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tanmanas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tanmanas" class="skt-link">f.</a> ayant cela à l'esprit; préoccupé par cela.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanmaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanmaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tanmaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tanmaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tanmayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tanmayī</i> fait de cela, de sa matière, identique à lui; absorbé dans; consubstantiel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanmayataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanmayatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-tanmayataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> absorption dans, identification avec; consubstantialité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanmayatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanmayatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-tanmayatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-tanmayataa" class="skt-link">tanmayatā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanmayiibhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanmayībhava</i> [<a href="#entry-bhava" class="skt-link">bhava</a>] <a href="#entry-tanmayiibhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de devenir semblable à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanmayiibhavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanmayībhavana</i> [<a href="#entry-bhavana" class="skt-link">bhavana</a>] <a href="#entry-tanmayiibhavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> identification<br> <a href="#entry-tanmaya" class="skt-link">tanmaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tannimitta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tannimitta</i> [<a href="#entry-nimitta" class="skt-link">nimitta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tannimitta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tannimitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tannimittaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tannimittā</i> causé par cela <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> relatif à cela; conforme à cela.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tannimittatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tannimittatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-tannimittatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> le fait d'en être la cause.<br> <a href="#entry-tannimitta" class="skt-link">tannimitta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्ति</span> <i>tanti</i> var. <i>tantī</i> [<a href="#entry-tan_1" class="skt-link">tan_1</a><a href="#entry--ti" class="skt-link">-ti</a>] <a href="#entry-tanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> corde; longe; ligne de longes dans une étable — <a href="#entry-tanti" class="skt-link">m.</a> tisserand.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantipaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantipāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-tantipaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Tantipāla «Gardien de la longe», fausse identité de <a href="#entry-Usahadeva" class="skt-link">Sahadeva</a> déguisé en maître des étables, pour se cacher à la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a> lors de la 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ année d'exil des <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tanti" class="skt-link">tanti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्त्र</span> <i>tantra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-tan_1" class="skt-link">tan_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> fil; chaîne (d'un tissu); trame; continuité, succession <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> règle, méthode; traité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. not. ouvrage sectaire <a href="#entry-zaakta" class="skt-link">śākta</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. programme, logiciel — <a href="#entry-tantrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tantrī</i> corde d'un luth <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vaisseau du corps — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(tantrayati)</i> maintenir dans l'ordre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> régir, appliquer une méthode, exécuter un <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantraakhyaayika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantrākhyāyika</i> [<a href="#entry-aakhyaayika" class="skt-link">ākhyāyika</a>] <a href="#entry-tantraakhyaayika" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tantrākhyāyika, version ancienne du <a href="#entry-Upa_ncatantra" class="skt-link">Pañcatantra</a> du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantraaloka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantrāloka</i> [<a href="#entry-aaloka" class="skt-link">āloka</a>] <a href="#entry-tantraaloka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tantrāloka, ouvrage phil. majeur d'<a href="#entry-Uabhinavagupta" class="skt-link">Abhinavagupta</a>; il traite not. du rituel du sacrifice de transgression [<a href="#entry-kulayaaga" class="skt-link">kulayāga</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tantraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tantraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tantrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tantrikā</i> neuf (se dit d'un tissu juste retiré de la trame).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantrarahasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantrarahasya</i> [<a href="#entry-rahasya" class="skt-link">rahasya</a>] <a href="#entry-tantrarahasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Tantrarahasya de <a href="#entry-Uraamaanujaacaarya" class="skt-link">Rāmānujācārya</a>, qui élabore la <a href="#entry-Umiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">Mīmāṃsā</a> selon <a href="#entry-Uprabhaakara" class="skt-link">Prabhākara</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uzaastraprameyapariccheda" class="skt-link">Śāstraprameyapariccheda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantrasaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantrasāra</i> [<a href="#entry-saara" class="skt-link">sāra</a>] <a href="#entry-tantrasaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tantrasāra «Essence du <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>», ouvrage d'<a href="#entry-Uabhinavagupta" class="skt-link">Abhinavagupta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantrasadbhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantrasadbhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-sadbhaava" class="skt-link">sadbhāva</a>] <a href="#entry-tantrasadbhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tantrasadbhāva, ouvrage de la doctrine <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantrasafgraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantrasaṃgraha</i> [<a href="#entry-safgraha" class="skt-link">saṃgraha</a>] <a href="#entry-tantrasa_mgraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tantrasaṃgraha, traité de mathématiques et d'astronomie dû à <a href="#entry-Uniilaka_n_tha_3" class="skt-link">Nīlakaṇṭha_3</a> <a href="#entry-Usomayaajii" class="skt-link">Somayājī</a> (1500), en 432 versets; il décrit un modèle planétaire héliocentrique où les 5 planètes tournent autour du Soleil, lui-même tournant autour de la Terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantrasamuccaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantrasamuccaya</i> [<a href="#entry-samuccaya" class="skt-link">samuccaya</a>] <a href="#entry-tantrasamuccaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tantrasamuccaya «Bréviaire tantrique», manuel de rituel du 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle utilisé au <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantrava_tadhaanikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantravaṭadhānikā</i> [<a href="#entry-va_ta" class="skt-link">vaṭa</a>-<a href="#entry-dhaanikaa" class="skt-link">dhānikā</a>] <a href="#entry-tantrava_tadhaanikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Tantravaṭadhānikā, ouvrage d'<a href="#entry-Uabhinavagupta" class="skt-link">Abhinavagupta</a> résumé de son <a href="#entry-Utantraaloka" class="skt-link">Tantrāloka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantravaarttika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantravārttika</i> [<a href="#entry-vaarttika" class="skt-link">vārttika</a>] <a href="#entry-tantravaarttika" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Tantravārttika, commentaire par <a href="#entry-Ukumaarila" class="skt-link">Kumārila</a> du <a href="#entry-Upuurvamiimaa_msaasuutra" class="skt-link">Pūrvamīmāṃsāsūtra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-U_tup_tiikaa" class="skt-link">Ṭupṭīkā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantrayukti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantrayukti</i> [<a href="#entry-yukti" class="skt-link">yukti</a>] <a href="#entry-tantrayukti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. procédé rhétorique; <a href="#entry-Ukau_tilya" class="skt-link">Kauṭilya</a> en énumère 32 dans l'<a href="#entry-Uarthazaastra" class="skt-link">Arthaśāstra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> validité scientifique des textes traditionnels.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantrin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्त्रिन्</span> <i>tantrin</i> [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tantrin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tantrin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tantri_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tantriṇī</i> qui a des fils; fait de fils, filé, tissé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à cordes (instrument) — <a href="#entry-tantrin" class="skt-link">m.</a> musicien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्तु</span> <i>tantu</i> [<a href="#entry-tan_1" class="skt-link">tan_1</a><a href="#entry--tu" class="skt-link">-tu</a>] <a href="#entry-tantu" class="skt-link">m.</a> fil, ficelle; cable; filament, fibre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lignage; continuité.<br> <i>tantu</i> <i>kṛ</i> filer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantukara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantukaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-tantukara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> filage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantukriyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantukriyā</i> [<a href="#entry-kriyaa" class="skt-link">kriyā</a>] <a href="#entry-tantukriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> travail de filage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantuparvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantuparvan</i> [<a href="#entry-parvan" class="skt-link">parvan</a>] <a href="#entry-tantuparvan" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. festival du cordon, célébrant l'anniversaire de l'<a href="#entry-upanayana" class="skt-link">upanayana</a> de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> [syn. <a href="#entry-pavitraaropa_na" class="skt-link">pavitrāropaṇa</a>]; on le fête le 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-zraava_na_2" class="skt-link">śrāvaṇa_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantutva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्तुत्व</span> <i>tantutva</i> [<a href="#entry-tantu" class="skt-link">tantu</a><a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-tantutva" class="skt-link">n.</a> fibrosité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tantuvaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tantuvāya</i> [<a href="#entry-vaaya" class="skt-link">vāya</a>] <a href="#entry-tantuvaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> tisserand.<br> <a href="#entry-tantu" class="skt-link">tantu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तनु</span> <i>tanu</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-tanuu" class="skt-link">tanū</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanu_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तनु</span> <i>tanu_1</i> [<a href="#entry-tan_1" class="skt-link">tan_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tanu_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tanu_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tanuu" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tanū</i> <a href="#entry-tanvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tanvī</i> mince, svelte, élancé; ténu, léger, fin, maigre; clair (liquide); subtil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faible, peu important, négligeable — <a href="#entry-tanvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tanvī</i> [élancée] jolie femme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>tenuis</i>; all. dünn; ang. thin; fr. ténu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanubh_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanubhṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_rt" class="skt-link">bhṛt</a>] <a href="#entry-tanubh_rt" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui a un corps»] être vivant; homme.<br> <a href="#entry-tanu" class="skt-link">tanu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanuja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanuja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] <a href="#entry-tanuja" class="skt-link">m.</a> fils — <a href="#entry-tanujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tanujā</i> fille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanujatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanujatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-tanujatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> qualité de fils.<br> <a href="#entry-tanuja" class="skt-link">tanuja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanumaanasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanumānasa</i> [<a href="#entry-maanasa" class="skt-link">mānasa</a>] <a href="#entry-tanumaanasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [YV.] l'affinement de l'esprit (par concentration sur un objet), 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ niveau de connaissance [<a href="#entry-j_naanabhuumikaa" class="skt-link">jñānabhūmikā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-tanu_1" class="skt-link">tanu_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanumadhyama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanumadhyama</i> [<a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">madhyama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tanumadhyama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tanumadhyama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tanumadhyamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tanumadhyamā</i> à la taille fine, aux hanches fines.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तनू</span> <i>tanū</i> [f. <a href="#entry-tanu_1" class="skt-link">tanu_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tanuu" class="skt-link">f.</a> corps; personne, soi-même — <a href="#entry-tanus" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>tanus</i> id. — <a href="#entry-tanu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>tanu</i> ifc. dont le corps est<br> <i>tanū</i> <i>kṛ</i> rétrécir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanuunapaat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tanūnapāt</i> [<a href="#entry-napaat" class="skt-link">napāt</a>] <a href="#entry-tanuunapaat" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. [«fils de son propre corps»] np. de Tanūnapāt «Auto-engendré», épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tanuu" class="skt-link">tanū</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tanyatu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्यतु</span> <i>tanyatu</i> [<a href="#entry-tan_2" class="skt-link">tan_2</a>] <a href="#entry-tanyatu" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. tonnerre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तप्</span> <i>tap</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(tapati)</i> pr. md. <i>(tapate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tapita</i>, <i>tapta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> avoir chaud, chauffer, brûler; tourmenter, faire souffrir; pratiquer l'ascétisme — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(tapyate)</i> s'échauffer; souffrir, se livrer à l'ascèse — ps. <i>(tapyate)</i> être chauffé, être tourmenté — ca. <i>(tāpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tāpita</i><i>)</i> faire souffrir, brûler — int. md. <i>(tātapyate)</i> brûler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>tepeo</i>; ang. tepid; fr. tiède.<br> <i>paścāt</i> <i>tap</i> se repentir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तप</span> <i>tapa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] <a href="#entry-tapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> chaleur; saison chaude — ifc. qui tourmente.<br> <i>paraṃtapa</i> a. m. n. qui tourmente son ennemi; épith. de héros, not. d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तपन</span> <i>tapana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tapana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tapana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tapanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tapanā</i> qui chauffe, qui brûle, qui brille — <a href="#entry-tapana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Tapana «Brûlant», épith. de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तपस्</span> <i>tapas</i> [<a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] <a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">n.</a> chaleur, ardeur; souffrance, tourment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mortification, austérités, pénitences, ascèse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. la force d'âme acquise par l'ascèse, une des disciplines [<a href="#entry-niyama" class="skt-link">niyama</a>] du <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> observance qui incombe à la caste — <a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. nom du mois véd. <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">māgha</a> (janvier-février) — ind. <i>tapar</i> [<i>taparloka</i>] myth. np. de Tapar «Ciel des ascètes», 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ monde céleste [<a href="#entry-loka" class="skt-link">loka</a>], où demeurent les renonçants [<a href="#entry-vairaagin" class="skt-link">vairāgin</a>] — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(tapasyati)</i> pratiquer l'ascèse, se macérer.<br> <i>tapasā</i> i. adv. par la pénitence.<br> <i>pañca</i> <i>tapāṃsi</i> soc. les 5 feux de pénitence: 4 feux en carré autour de l'ascète, et le Soleil au dessus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapaskara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tapaskara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tapaskara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. marquage au fer rouge, un <a href="#entry-sa_mskaara" class="skt-link">saṃskāra</a> d'initiation vishnouïte; syn. <a href="#entry-taptasa_mskaara" class="skt-link">taptasaṃskāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapasvin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तपस्विन्</span> <i>tapasvin</i> [<a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a><a href="#entry--vin" class="skt-link">-vin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tapasvin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tapasvin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tapasvinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tapasvinī</i> qui pratique des austérités, ascétique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pieux; austère; tourmenté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> souffrant, misérable, malheureux, pitoyable — <a href="#entry-tapasvin" class="skt-link">m.</a> ascète, ermite — <a href="#entry-tapasvinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tapasvinī</i> pauvre femme; femme ascète <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Tapasvinī «Ascétique», mère de <a href="#entry-Umaatali" class="skt-link">Mātali</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तपस्य</span> <i>tapasya</i> [<a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-tapasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> pratique d'austérités, ascèse, discipline spirituelle — <a href="#entry-tapasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. nom d'un mois véd. (<a href="#entry-phaalguna" class="skt-link">phālguna</a>, février-mars).<br> <i>tapasye</i> <i>bhū</i> se mettre à l'ascèse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तपत्</span> <i>tapat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tapat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tapat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tapantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tapantī</i> chauffant, brûlant — <a href="#entry-tapatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tapatī</i> myth. np. de la Tapatī «Canicule», fille de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a> et <a href="#entry-Uchaayaa" class="skt-link">Chāyā</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Usa_mvara_na" class="skt-link">Saṃvaraṇa</a> et mère de <a href="#entry-Ukuru" class="skt-link">Kuru</a>; elle personnifie une rivière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तपित</span> <i>tapita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tapita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tapita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tapitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tapitā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tapta" class="skt-link">tapta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapodhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tapodhana</i> [<a href="#entry-dhana" class="skt-link">dhana</a>] <a href="#entry-tapodhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> ascète (qui n'a pour richesse que ses austérités).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapovana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tapovana</i> [<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] <a href="#entry-tapovana" class="skt-link">m.</a> ermitage [«bois d'ascèse»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a> Tapovana <a href="#entry-Umahaaraaja" class="skt-link">Mahārāja</a> «Ascète Suprême» (1889–1957); il fut le <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> de <a href="#entry-Ucinmayaananda" class="skt-link">Cinmayānanda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tapta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तप्त</span> <i>tapta</i> var. <i>tapita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tapta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tapta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taptā</i> chauffé; chaud, ardent; incandescent, rougi au feu; fondu, en fusion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tourmenté, affligé; qui a pratiqué des pénitences.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taptasa_mskaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taptasaṃskāra</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mskaara" class="skt-link">saṃskāra</a>] <a href="#entry-taptasa_mskaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. marquage au fer rouge, un <a href="#entry-sa_mskaara" class="skt-link">saṃskāra</a> d'initiation vishnouïte; syn. <a href="#entry-tapaskara" class="skt-link">tapaskara</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tapta" class="skt-link">tapta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्ष</span> <i>tarṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-t_r_s_1" class="skt-link">tṛṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> désir; soif — ifc. désir de — <a href="#entry-tar_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarṣā</i> désir; soif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tar_sam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्षम्</span> <i>tarṣam</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-t_r_s_1" class="skt-link">tṛṣ_1</a>] ind. ayant été assoiffé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर</span> <i>tara</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarī</i> qui fait traverser, passeur; qui sauve — ifc. fait de surmonter — <a href="#entry-tara" class="skt-link">m.</a> traversée, passage — <a href="#entry-tarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarī</i> bateau, navire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तरण</span> <i>taraṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] <a href="#entry-tara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> radeau, barque — <a href="#entry-tara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> traversée (de ⟨iic.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de surmonter (un obstacle, une infortune).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तरंग</span> <i>taraṃga</i> var. <i>taraṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-taram" class="skt-link">taram</a>-<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tara_mga" class="skt-link">m.</a> vague, onde; remous; va et vient <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. chapitre d'un ouvrage, not. <a href="#entry-Ukathaasaritsaagara" class="skt-link">Kathāsaritsāgara</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uraajatarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">Rājataraṅgiṇī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarafgin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taraṃgin</i> var. <i>taraṅgin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tara_mgin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tara_mgin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tara_mgi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taraṃgiṇī</i> qui ondule, alternatif; qui va et vient sans cesse — <a href="#entry-tarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taraṅgiṇī</i> rivière — ifc. lit. forme des noms d'ouvrages, not. biographies.<br> <a href="#entry-tarafga" class="skt-link">taraṃga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarafgita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taraṃgita</i> var. <i>taraṅgita</i> [<a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tara_mgita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tara_mgita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tara_mgitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taraṃgitā</i> qui va et vient; qui ondule; noyé (de larmes) — <a href="#entry-tara_mgita" class="skt-link">n.</a> mouvement de vagues, de va et vient.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तरल</span> <i>tarala</i> a. <a href="#entry-tarala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tarala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taralaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taralā</i> tremblant; mobile <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> étincelant — <a href="#entry-tarala" class="skt-link">m.</a> vague; collier; rubis — <a href="#entry-taralaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taralā</i> liqueur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taralikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taralikā</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-taralikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. de Taralikā, compagne de <a href="#entry-Umahaazvetaa" class="skt-link">Mahāśvetā</a> utilisée comme entremetteuse par <a href="#entry-Upu_n_dariika_2" class="skt-link">Puṇḍarīka_2</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tarala" class="skt-link">tarala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तरम्</span> <i>taram</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-tara" class="skt-link">tara</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarambuja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तरम्बुज</span> <i>tarambuja</i> <a href="#entry-tarambuja" class="skt-link">n.</a> pastèque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तरस्</span> <i>taras</i> <a href="#entry-taras" class="skt-link">n.</a> vitesse, vélocité; force, énergie, efficacité — a. <a href="#entry-taras" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taras" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taras" class="skt-link">f.</a> rapide; énergique.<br> <i>tarasā</i> adv. rapidement, directement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarasvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarasvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tarasvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tarasvat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tarasvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarasvatī</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tarasvin" class="skt-link">tarasvin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarasvin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarasvin</i> [<a href="#entry--vin" class="skt-link">-vin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tarasvin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tarasvin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tarasvinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarasvinī</i> rapide, violent, énergique; hardi — <a href="#entry-tarasvin" class="skt-link">m.</a> messager; coureur; héros <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faucon; épith. de <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-taras" class="skt-link">taras</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taratama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तरतम</span> <i>taratama</i> de <i>-tara</i>, <i>-tama</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्हि</span> <i>tarhi</i> adv. [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yadi" class="skt-link">yadi</a>] alors, maintenant, en ce moment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> par conséquent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarhyeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarhyeva</i> [<a href="#entry-eva" class="skt-link">eva</a>] adv. alors seulement.<br> <a href="#entry-tarhi" class="skt-link">tarhi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तरि</span> <i>tari</i> [<a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] <a href="#entry-tari" class="skt-link">f.</a> bateau; not. barque de passeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>tarī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarika</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-tarika" class="skt-link">m.</a> passeur.<br> <a href="#entry-tari" class="skt-link">tari</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्ज्</span> <i>tarj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(tarjati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tarjita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> menacer, terrifier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gronder, blâmer; se moquer de, ridiculiser — ca. <i>(tarjayati)</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarjaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्जक</span> <i>tarjaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tarj" class="skt-link">tarj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tarjaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tarjaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tarjikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarjikā</i> qui menace.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarjana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्जन</span> <i>tarjana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tarj" class="skt-link">tarj</a>] <a href="#entry-tarjana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de menacer, de gronder, de ridiculiser — <a href="#entry-tarjanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarjanī</i> (doigt qui menace) index <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> attitude menaçante (not. d'images sacrées).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarjita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्जित</span> <i>tarjita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tarj" class="skt-link">tarj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tarjita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tarjita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tarjitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarjitā</i> menacé; grondé, humilié; ridiculisé — <a href="#entry-tarjita" class="skt-link">n.</a> menace.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tark">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्क्</span> <i>tark</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(tarkayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tarkita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> considérer, examiner, apprécier, évaluer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> réfléchir, juger; conjecturer; soupçonner, douter de ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. τρεπω; lat. <i>torqueo</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्क</span> <i>tarka</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tark" class="skt-link">tark</a>] <a href="#entry-tarka" class="skt-link">m.</a> sujet de réflexion, de discussion; topique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> supposition, conjecture; spéculation, examen, évaluation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> discussion, délibération <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> raison; raisonnement, argumentation, réflexion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. logique, dialectique; not. raisonnement par l'absurde, réfutation — <a href="#entry-tarkaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarkā</i> phil. raisonnement; investigation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarka_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्कणीय</span> <i>tarkaṇīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-tark" class="skt-link">tark</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tarka_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tarka_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tarka_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarkaṇīyā</i> suspect.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkaalafkaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkālaṅkāra</i> [<a href="#entry-alafkaara" class="skt-link">alaṅkāra</a>] <a href="#entry-tarkaalafkaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du logicien Rāma Tarkālaṅkāra (16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), élève de <a href="#entry-Uraamabhadra_1" class="skt-link">Rāmabhadra_1</a> <a href="#entry-Usaarvabhauma" class="skt-link">Sārvabhauma</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Umathuraanaatha_1" class="skt-link">Mathurānātha_1</a>; il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Uvi_sayataavaada" class="skt-link">Viṣayatāvāda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tarka" class="skt-link">tarka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkadiipikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkadīpikā</i> [<a href="#entry-diipikaa" class="skt-link">dīpikā</a>] <a href="#entry-tarkadiipikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] np. de la Tarkadīpikā «Exégèse de logique», traité d'<a href="#entry-Uannambha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Annambhaṭṭa</a> glosant son formulaire <a href="#entry-Utarkasafgraha" class="skt-link">Tarkasaṃgraha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkakaumudii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkakaumudī</i> [<a href="#entry-kaumudii" class="skt-link">kaumudī</a>] <a href="#entry-tarkakaumudii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] np. de la Tarkakaumudī «Lumière sur la logique», œuvre de <a href="#entry-Ulaugaak_si" class="skt-link">Laugākṣi</a> Bhāskara_2.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkapaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkapāda</i> [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] <a href="#entry-tarkapaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. section d'un ouvrage traitant de la discussion rationnelle du sujet propre de l'ouvrage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkasafgraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkasaṃgraha</i> [<a href="#entry-safgraha" class="skt-link">saṃgraha</a>] <a href="#entry-tarkasa_mgraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] np. du Tarkasaṃgraha «Résumé de logique», traité-formulaire d'<a href="#entry-Uannambha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Annambhaṭṭa</a>; la <a href="#entry-Utarkadiipikaa" class="skt-link">Tarkadīpikā</a> du même auteur en est une glose; la <a href="#entry-Unyaayabodhinii" class="skt-link">Nyāyabodhinī</a> et le <a href="#entry-Upadak_rtya" class="skt-link">Padakṛtya</a> en sont des commentaires.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkataa_n_dava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkatāṇḍava</i> [<a href="#entry-taa_n_dava" class="skt-link">tāṇḍava</a>] <a href="#entry-tarkataa_n_dava" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tarkatāṇḍava «Danse de logique», ouvrage de <a href="#entry-Uvyaasatiirtha" class="skt-link">Vyāsatīrtha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkavaacaspati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkavācaspati</i> [<a href="#entry-vaacaspati" class="skt-link">vācaspati</a>] <a href="#entry-tarkavaacaspati" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Tārānātha Tarkavācaspati, (1806–1885), érudit bengali, lexicographe, supérieur du Sanskrit College de Calcutta, auteur du <a href="#entry-Uvaacaspatya" class="skt-link">Vācaspatya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkavaagiiza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkavāgīśa</i> [<a href="#entry-vaagiiza" class="skt-link">vāgīśa</a>] <a href="#entry-tarkavaagiiza" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-Umathuraanaatha_1" class="skt-link">Mathurānātha_1</a> Tarkavāgīśa, fils et élève de Rāma <a href="#entry-Utarkaalafkaara" class="skt-link">Tarkālaṅkāra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Umathuraanaatha_1" class="skt-link">Mathurānātha_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkavidyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkavidyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-tarkavidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. science du raisonnement; syn. <a href="#entry-tarkazaastra" class="skt-link">tarkaśāstra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-tarkazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. science du raisonnement; cf. <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्किन्</span> <i>tarkin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tark" class="skt-link">tark</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tarkin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tarkin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tarki_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarkiṇī</i> imbu de spéculation, versé en logique; logicien <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. dialecticien; sceptique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarku">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्कु</span> <i>tarku</i> [<a href="#entry-tark" class="skt-link">tark</a>] <a href="#entry-tarku" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tarku" class="skt-link">n.</a> fuseau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkukarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tarkukarman</i> <a href="#entry-tarkukarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> pl. <i>tarkukarmāṇi</i> les travaux de filage, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-tarku" class="skt-link">tarku</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarkya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्क्य</span> <i>tarkya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-tark" class="skt-link">tark</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tarkya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tarkya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tarkyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tarkyā</i> à considérer, à évaluer, à examiner; qui mérite réflexion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> douteux, suspect.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarp">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्प्</span> <i>tarp</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-t_rp_1" class="skt-link">tṛp_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(tarpayati)</i> satisfaire, contenter, réjouir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tarpa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्पण</span> <i>tarpaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tarp" class="skt-link">tarp</a>] <a href="#entry-tarpa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> satisfaction, satiété <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. libation d'eau aux mânes — ifc. fait de contenter; nourriture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तरु</span> <i>taru</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-daaru" class="skt-link">dāru</a>] <a href="#entry-taru" class="skt-link">m.</a> arbre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. tree.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taru_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तरुण</span> <i>taruṇa</i> a. <a href="#entry-taru_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-taru_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-taru_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taruṇī</i> jeune, juvénil, vigoureux; frais, tendre, délicat; juste commencé — <a href="#entry-taru_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> adolescent, jouvenceau — <a href="#entry-taru_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taruṇī</i> jeune fille — voc. <i>taruṇi</i> adr. Mademoiselle — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(taruṇayati)</i> rajeunir — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(taruṇāyate)</i> être jeune; rester jeune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. τερην.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taru_sa_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>taruṣaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-_sa_n_da" class="skt-link">ṣaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-taru_sa_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> bosquet, parc.<br> <a href="#entry-taru" class="skt-link">taru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tasil">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तसिल्</span> <i>tasil</i> nota. gram. suffixe ablatif, obtenu par affixation de <a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-taskara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तस्कर</span> <i>taskara</i> <a href="#entry-taskara" class="skt-link">m.</a> voleur, brigand.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tasmaat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तस्मात्</span> <i>tasmāt</i> [abl. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] adv. [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yasmaat" class="skt-link">yasmāt</a>] ainsi, pour cette raison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tasmai">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तस्मै</span> <i>tasmai</i> [dat. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] ind. à lui, à ce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tasmin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तस्मिन्</span> <i>tasmin</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] ind. en lui, dans ce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tasthivas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तस्थिवस्</span> <i>tasthivas</i> [ppft. <a href="#entry-sthaa_1" class="skt-link">sthā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tasthivas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tasthivas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tasthu_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tasthuṣī</i> s'étant tenu, qui s'est tenu, qui se tenait; qui continue; prêt à ⟨dat.⟩ — <a href="#entry-tasthivat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>tasthivat</i> le règne végétal et minéral; l'immobile (opp. <a href="#entry-jagat" class="skt-link">jagat</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. l'immuable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तस्य</span> <i>tasya</i> [g. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] ind. de lui, de ce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tasyaam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तस्याम्</span> <i>tasyām</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-saa_2" class="skt-link">sā_2</a>] ind. en elle, sur elle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tasyaas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तस्यास्</span> <i>tasyās</i> [g. <a href="#entry-saa_2" class="skt-link">sā_2</a>] ind. d'elle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tasyai">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तस्यै</span> <i>tasyai</i> [dat. <a href="#entry-saa_2" class="skt-link">sā_2</a>] ind. à elle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तत्</span> <i>tat</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tata_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तत</span> <i>tata_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tan_1" class="skt-link">tan_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tata_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tata_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatā</i> étendu, tendu, bandé (arc); étalé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accompli (sacrifice).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tata_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तत</span> <i>tata_2</i> <a href="#entry-tata_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. père, papa — voc. adr. petit père, mon petit, mon chéri.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ततस्</span> <i>tatas</i> [<a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a><a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. substitut de l'abl. de <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a> ou d'un autre pronom <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yatas" class="skt-link">yatas</a>] d'ici, à partir d'ici <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ensuite, après; désormais <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> donc, ainsi, par conséquent, c'est pourquoi, il s'ensuit que.<br> <i>itas</i> <i>tatas</i> adv. ça et là, en telle ou telle circonstance.<br> <i>tatas</i> <i>tatas</i> adv. de côté et d'autre.<br> <i>yatas</i> <i>...</i> <i>tatas</i> <i>...</i> comme ... il s'ensuit que ...<br> <i>tasmai</i> <i>deyaṃ</i> <i>tato</i> <i>grāhyam</i> On doit lui faire des offrandes, on peut accepter ses dons.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तथा</span> <i>tathā</i> [<a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a><a href="#entry--thaa" class="skt-link">-thā</a>] adv. [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yathaa" class="skt-link">yathā</a>] ainsi, de cette manière; aussi, de même <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (part. d'assentiment) oui; d'accord; soit! — iic. de tel ...; ainsi ....<br> <i>tathā</i> <i>bhavatu</i> var. <i>tathāstu</i> ainsi soit-il.<br> <i>tathā</i> <i>kuru</i> fais ainsi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaabhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathābhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">bhāva</a>] <a href="#entry-tathaabhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> le fait étant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le fait d'être de telle nature; la vraie nature; l'accomplissement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaabhaavin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathābhāvin</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaavin" class="skt-link">bhāvin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tathaabhaavin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaabhaavin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaabhaavinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tathābhāvinī</i> entrant dans tel état.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaabhuuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathābhūta</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tathaabhuuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaabhuuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaabhuutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tathābhūtā</i> de telle nature, de telle essence, de telle nature; ayant de telles qualités.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaaca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathāca</i> [<a href="#entry-ca" class="skt-link">ca</a>] adv. même ainsi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaagata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathāgata</i> [<a href="#entry-gata" class="skt-link">gata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tathaagata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaagata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaagataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tathāgatā</i> de cette nature; parvenu de cette manière — <a href="#entry-tathaagata" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> «l'ainsi venu» (venu comme ses prédécesseurs).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaagatabhadra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathāgatabhadra</i> [<a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">bhadra</a>] <a href="#entry-tathaagatabhadra" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de Tathāgatabhadra, élève de <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tathaagata" class="skt-link">tathāgata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaagatagarbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathāgatagarbha</i> [<a href="#entry-garbha" class="skt-link">garbha</a>] <a href="#entry-tathaagatagarbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. doctrine <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a> qui postule que tout être est un <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> en germe; cf. <a href="#entry-Uratnagotravibhaaga" class="skt-link">Ratnagotravibhāga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaak_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathākṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">kṛta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tathaak_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaak_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaak_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tathākṛtā</i> ainsi fait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaamukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathāmukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tathaamukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaamukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaamukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tathāmukhī</i> ainsi orienté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> regardant dans la même direction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaapi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathāpi</i> [<a href="#entry-api" class="skt-link">api</a>] adv. néanmoins, cependant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yadyapi" class="skt-link">yadyapi</a>] bien que ... pourtant ...<br> <i>yadyapi</i> <i>ayaṃ</i> <i>bahu</i> <i>prayāsaṃ</i> <i>kṛtavān</i> <i>tathāpi</i> <i>parīkṣāyām</i> <i>anuttīrṇaḥ</i> bien qu'il ait fait beaucoup d'efforts, il a échoué à l'examen.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaaruupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathārūpa</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tathaaruupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaaruupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaaruupaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tathārūpā</i> de telle forme; paraissant ainsi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaaruupin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathārūpin</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupin" class="skt-link">rūpin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tathaaruupin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaaruupin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tathaaruupi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tathārūpiṇī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathātā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-tathaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. vraie nature des choses.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathātva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-tathaatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. vraie nature des choses.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathaiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tathaiva</i> [<a href="#entry-eva" class="skt-link">eva</a>] adv. aussi, de même; de la même manière, exactement ainsi.<br> <i>tathaiva</i> <i>ca</i> adv. et de même; pareillement.<br> <a href="#entry-tathaa" class="skt-link">tathā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatheti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatheti</i> [<a href="#entry-iti" class="skt-link">iti</a>] adv. c'est bon, entendu, d'accord.<br> <i>bhavatu</i> <i>tatheti</i> <i>sa</i> <i>pratyuvāca</i> il répondit «très bien».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tathya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तथ्य</span> <i>tathya</i> [<a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tathya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tathya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tathyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tathyā</i> [«qui est ainsi»] vrai, réel; véridique — <a href="#entry-tathya" class="skt-link">n.</a> vérité, réalité.<br> <i>tathyena</i> adv. en vérité; réellement, pour de vrai.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तति</span> <i>tati</i> [<a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] conj. [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yati_1" class="skt-link">yati_1</a>] autant — <a href="#entry-tati" class="skt-link">f.</a> foule, quantité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatkara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatkara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatkara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatkara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatkaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatkarā</i> qui fait cela.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatkriya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatkriya</i> [<a href="#entry-kriyaa" class="skt-link">kriyā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatkriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatkriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatkriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatkriyā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tatkara" class="skt-link">tatkara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatpara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatpara</i> [<a href="#entry-para" class="skt-link">para</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatpara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatpara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatparaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatparā</i> qui le suit; qui lui est totalement dévoué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui s'adonne à ⟨loc. iic.⟩ — ifc. adepte de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatphala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatphala</i> [<a href="#entry-phala" class="skt-link">phala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatphala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatphala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatphalam" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatphalam</i> qui a ce résultat; qui obtient ce fruit ou cette récompense.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatprak_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatprakṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-prak_rtaa" class="skt-link">prakṛtā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatprak_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatprak_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatprak_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatprakṛtā</i> fait de ça.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatprakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatprakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-prakaara" class="skt-link">prakāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatprakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatprakaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatprakaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatprakārā</i> de cette espèce.<br> <i>tatprakāraka</i> id.<br> <i>tatprakārakaśabdabodhe</i> <i>śaktijñānasya</i> <i>hetutvam</i> La connaissance du sens est la cause de la compréhension des phrases.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatprathama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatprathama</i> [<a href="#entry-prathama" class="skt-link">prathama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatprathama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatprathama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatprathamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatprathamā</i> qui fait ça pour la première fois.<br> <i>tatprathamam</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tatpuurvam" class="skt-link">tatpūrvam</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatpuru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatpuruṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-puru_sa" class="skt-link">puruṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-tatpuru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. l'Esprit suprême <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-zaivasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">śaivasiddhānta</a>] np. de Tatpuruṣa, visage de majesté de <a href="#entry-Usadaaziva" class="skt-link">Sadāśiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Umahaadeva" class="skt-link">Mahādeva</a>, tourné vers l'Est, associé au monde de paix [<a href="#entry-zaanti" class="skt-link">śānti</a>], à l'élément air et à la couleur dorée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> son serviteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. mot composé déterminatif (comme le mot <a href="#entry-tatpuru_sa" class="skt-link">tatpuruṣa</a>); le 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ terme est déterminé par le 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩, par une relation casuelle ou par une relation attributive dans le cas d'un composé descriptif [<a href="#entry-karmadhaaraya" class="skt-link">karmadhāraya</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatpuurva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatpūrva</i> [<a href="#entry-puurva" class="skt-link">pūrva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatpuurva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatpuurva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatpuurvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatpūrvā</i> qui arrive pour la première fois.<br> <i>tatpūrvam</i> ainsi pour la première fois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तत्र</span> <i>tatra</i> [<a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a><a href="#entry--tra" class="skt-link">-tra</a>] adv. [corrél. de <a href="#entry-yatra" class="skt-link">yatra</a>] ici, là; en cela, à cela <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vers là — (en début de phrase) parmi ceux-ci, l'un d'eux.<br> <i>tatra</i> <i>kāle</i> adv. à ce moment là.<br> <i>tatraiva</i> adv. ici même.<br> <i>yatra</i> <i>tatra</i> adv. n'importe où.<br> <i>tatra</i> <i>tatra</i> adv. ici et là; partout.
  </div>
