</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatrabhavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatrabhavat</i> [<a href="#entry-bhavat_2" class="skt-link">bhavat_2</a>] <a href="#entry-tatrabhavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> adr. Monsieur (non-présent, forme polie de il) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. np. de Tatrabhavān «Monsieur», désignation respectueuse de <a href="#entry-Ubhart_rhari" class="skt-link">Bhartṛhari</a> par <a href="#entry-Uabhinavagupta" class="skt-link">Abhinavagupta</a> — <a href="#entry-tatrabhavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatrabhavatī</i> Madame.<br> <a href="#entry-tatra" class="skt-link">tatra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatratya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatratya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatratya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatratya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatratyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatratyā</i> qui est ici, qui est là.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatsad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatsad</i> [<a href="#entry-sad_1" class="skt-link">sad_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatsad" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatsad" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatsad" class="skt-link">f.</a> assis là.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatsama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tatsama</i> [<a href="#entry-sama" class="skt-link">sama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tatsama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tatsama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tatsamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tatsamā</i> similaire; pareil à lui; tel quel — <a href="#entry-tatsama" class="skt-link">m.</a> mot assimilé au sanskrit, sanskritisation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mot emprunté tel quel au sanskrit; cf. <a href="#entry-tadbhava" class="skt-link">tadbhava</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tat" class="skt-link">tat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तत्त्व</span> <i>tattva</i> [<a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a><a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">n.</a> réalité, vérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> essence, principe essentiel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] élément ou substance primaire; on en compte 24 ou 25: la <a href="#entry-prak_rti" class="skt-link">prakṛti</a>, et ses manifestations dans la création [<a href="#entry-sarga" class="skt-link">sarga</a>]: les 3 sens psychiques ou fonctions intellectuelles composant l'instrument interne [<a href="#entry-anta_hkara_na" class="skt-link">antaḥkaraṇa</a>] (<a href="#entry-buddhi" class="skt-link">buddhi</a>, <a href="#entry-ahafkaara" class="skt-link">ahaṃkāra</a>, <a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>, auxquels le <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a> rajoute <a href="#entry-citta" class="skt-link">citta</a> en tête), les 10 facultés de perception et d'action [<a href="#entry-indriya" class="skt-link">indriya</a>], les 5 éléments subtils [<a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">tanmātra</a>], et les 5 éléments grossiers [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>]; cette évolution [<a href="#entry-pari_naama" class="skt-link">pariṇāma</a>] des <i>tattvāni</i> se produit sous l'influence du <a href="#entry-puru_sa" class="skt-link">puruṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 25.<br> <i>tattvena</i> adv. en vérité, justement, exactement.<br> <i>ātmā</i> <i>tattvamasi</i> <i>śvetaketo</i> [ChU.] Tu es cet <a href="#entry-Uaatmaa" class="skt-link">Ātmā</a> même, Ô <a href="#entry-Uzvetaketu" class="skt-link">Śvetaketu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvaabhiniveza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvābhiniveśa</i> [<a href="#entry-abhiniveza" class="skt-link">abhiniveśa</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvaabhiniveza" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. imprégnation du sens de l'enseignement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvaanyatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvānyatva</i> [<a href="#entry-anyatva" class="skt-link">anyatva</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvaanyatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> du. de telle ou telle sorte.<br> <i>tattvānyatvābhyām</i> <i>anirvacanīya</i> qu'il est vain de chercher à préciser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> véracité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pertinence.<br> <a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvabindu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvabindu</i> [<a href="#entry-bindu" class="skt-link">bindu</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvabindu" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tattvabindu «Goutte de vérité», traité psycho-linguistique sur la perception du sens d'une énonciation verbale, dû à <a href="#entry-Uvaacaspati_1" class="skt-link">Vācaspati_1</a> <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a> (9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvabodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvabodha</i> [<a href="#entry-bodha" class="skt-link">bodha</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvabodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> connaissance de la vérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du Tattvabodha, ouvrage d'introduction au <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a> attribué à <a href="#entry-Uzafkaraacaarya" class="skt-link">Śaṅkarācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvacintaama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvacintāmaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-cintaama_ni" class="skt-link">cintāmaṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tattvacintāmaṇi «Joyau de la Vérité», ouvrage fondateur de l'école de logique de <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a> [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>], œuvre monumentale de <a href="#entry-Ugafgeza" class="skt-link">Gaṅgeśa</a> (14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il y expose la théorie des 4 moyens de connaissance [<a href="#entry-pramaa_na" class="skt-link">pramāṇa</a>], et propose 5 définitions [<a href="#entry-pa_ncalak_sa_nii" class="skt-link">pañcalakṣaṇī</a>] de la concomitance inévitable [<a href="#entry-vyaapti" class="skt-link">vyāpti</a>]; dans la section <a href="#entry-iizvaravaada" class="skt-link">īśvaravāda</a> il démontre l'existence de Dieu; ce texte est aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Upramaacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">Pramācintāmaṇi</a> and Maṇigrantha; il comprend 4 livres [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>], nommés Pratyakṣa, Anumāna, Upamāna et Śabda.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvacintaama_nidiidhiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvacintāmaṇidīdhiti</i> [<a href="#entry-diidhiti" class="skt-link">dīdhiti</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvacintaama_nidiidhiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Tattvacintāmaṇidīdhiti, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Utattvacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">Tattvacintāmaṇi</a>, œuvre principale de Raghunātha_1 <a href="#entry-Uziroma_ni" class="skt-link">Śiromaṇi</a>; <a href="#entry-Ujagadiiza_1" class="skt-link">Jagadīśa_1</a> en fit le commentaire Tattvacintāmaṇidīdhitiprakāśikā; cf. <a href="#entry-Ujaagadiizii" class="skt-link">Jāgadīśī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvacintaama_nimayuukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvacintāmaṇimayūkha</i> [<a href="#entry-mayuukha" class="skt-link">mayūkha</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvacintaama_nimayuukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tattvacintāmaṇimayūkha «Axe du Joyau de la Vérité», commentaire du <a href="#entry-Utattvacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">Tattvacintāmaṇi</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_2" class="skt-link">Śaṃkara_2</a> <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvacintaama_niprakaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvacintāmaṇiprakāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakaaza" class="skt-link">prakāśa</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvacintaama_niprakaaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tattvacintāmaṇiprakāśa, commentaire par <a href="#entry-Uvardhamaana_1" class="skt-link">Vardhamāna_1</a> du <a href="#entry-Utattvacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">Tattvacintāmaṇi</a> de son père.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvacintaama_nirahasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvacintāmaṇirahasya</i> [<a href="#entry-rahasya" class="skt-link">rahasya</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvacintaama_nirahasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Tattvacintāmaṇirahasya «Secret du <a href="#entry-Utattvacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">Tattvacintāmaṇi</a>», ouvrage de <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a> du logicien <a href="#entry-Umathuraanaatha_1" class="skt-link">Mathurānātha_1</a> [<a href="#entry-Umaathurii" class="skt-link">Māthurī</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-tattvacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">tattvacintāmaṇi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvaj_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvajñāna</i> [<a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">jñāna</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvaj_naana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. connaissance des principes essentiels.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvamiimaa_msaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvamīmāṃsā</i> [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvamiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Tattvamīmāṃsā «Investigation de la vérité», traité de <a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Udurbalaacaarya" class="skt-link">Durbalācārya</a>-<a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_namitraacaarya" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇamitrācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvanir_naya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvanirṇaya</i> [<a href="#entry-nir_naya" class="skt-link">nirṇaya</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvanir_naya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tattvanirṇaya de <a href="#entry-Umadhu" class="skt-link">Madhu</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Utattvasafkhyaana" class="skt-link">Tattvasaṅkhyāna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvaprakaazikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvaprakāśikā</i> [<a href="#entry-prakaazikaa" class="skt-link">prakāśikā</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvaprakaazikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Tattvaprakāśikā «Lumières sur le réel», commentaire du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutra" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtra</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a> de <a href="#entry-Umadhvaacaarya" class="skt-link">Madhvācārya</a>, dû à <a href="#entry-Ujayatiirtha" class="skt-link">Jayatīrtha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvaratnaavalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvaratnāvalī</i> [<a href="#entry-ratnaavalii" class="skt-link">ratnāvalī</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvaratnaavalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Tattvaratnāvalī de <a href="#entry-Umaitriipa" class="skt-link">Maitrīpa</a>, incorporant des rites tantriques à la doctrine bd. <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvasafgraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvasaṃgraha</i> [<a href="#entry-safgraha" class="skt-link">saṃgraha</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvasa_mgraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tattvasaṃgraha, encyclopédie philosophique en vers de <a href="#entry-Uzaantarak_sita" class="skt-link">Śāntarakṣita</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvasafgrahapa_njikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvasaṃgrahapañjikā</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_njikaa" class="skt-link">pañjikā</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvasa_mgrahapa_njikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Tattvasaṃgrahapañjikā, commentaire analytique du <a href="#entry-Utattvasafgraha" class="skt-link">Tattvasaṃgraha</a> par <a href="#entry-Ukamalaziila" class="skt-link">Kamalaśīla</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tattvasafgraha" class="skt-link">tattvasaṃgraha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvasafkhyaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvasaṅkhyāna</i> [<a href="#entry-safkhyaana" class="skt-link">saṅkhyāna</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvasafkhyaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Tattvasaṅkhyāna, manifeste philosophique de <a href="#entry-Umadhu" class="skt-link">Madhu</a> donnant ses vues sur le <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>; il insiste sur 5 dualités [<a href="#entry-dvaita" class="skt-link">dvaita</a>] fondamentales, dont son système dérive son nom; aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Utattvanir_naya" class="skt-link">Tattvanirṇaya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvasamaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvasamāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-samaasa" class="skt-link">samāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvasamaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Tattvasamāsa, manuel de philosaphie <a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>, attribué à <a href="#entry-Ukapila" class="skt-link">Kapila</a>; il est antérieur à 1600.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> vérité; réalité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvatas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvatas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. par la vérité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. doctrine de dualisme [<a href="#entry-dvaita" class="skt-link">dvaita</a>] de <a href="#entry-Umadhvaacaarya" class="skt-link">Madhvācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvavaizaaradii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvavaiśāradī</i> [<a href="#entry-vaizaaradii" class="skt-link">vaiśāradī</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvavaizaaradii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Tattvavaiśāradī, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Upaata_njalayogazaastra" class="skt-link">Pātañjalayogaśāstra</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uvaacaspati_1" class="skt-link">Vācaspati_1</a> <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvavid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvavid</i> [<a href="#entry-vid_3" class="skt-link">vid_3</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tattvavid" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tattvavid" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tattvavid" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui connaît la nature de ⟨g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tattvaviveka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tattvaviveka</i> [<a href="#entry-viveka" class="skt-link">viveka</a>] <a href="#entry-tattvaviveka" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] «discernement de la réalité», doctrine moniste [<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a>] enseignant que seul l'<a href="#entry-Uaatmaa" class="skt-link">Ātmā</a> est réel.<br> <i>ātmā</i> <i>satyastadanyat</i> <i>sarvamithyeti</i> [<a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-Uaatmaa" class="skt-link">Ātmā</a> existe, tout le reste est illusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तत्व</span> <i>tatva</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tau">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तौ</span> <i>tau</i> [nom. du. <a href="#entry-sa_2" class="skt-link">sa_2</a>] ind. eux deux, ces deux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तव</span> <i>tava</i> [g. <a href="#entry-tvad" class="skt-link">tvad</a>] ind. de toi, à toi, tien.<br> <i>tavāsmi</i> [«je suis à toi»] je me rends (parole de reddition d'un vaincu).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tavarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तवर्ग</span> <i>tavarga</i> [<i>(ta)</i>-<a href="#entry-varga" class="skt-link">varga</a>] <a href="#entry-tavarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. la classe des dentales.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tavas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तवस्</span> <i>tavas</i> a. <a href="#entry-tavas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tavas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tavas" class="skt-link">f.</a> fort, énergique; courageux — <a href="#entry-tavas" class="skt-link">m.</a> puissance, force, courage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tavi_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तविष</span> <i>taviṣa</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-tavas" class="skt-link">tavas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tavi_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tavi_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tavi_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taviṣī</i> fort, énergique, courageux — <a href="#entry-tavi_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> force, courage — <a href="#entry-tavi_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>taviṣī</i> id. — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(taviṣīyati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(taviṣīyate)</i> être puissant; montrer du courage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tavyat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तव्यत्</span> <i>tavyat</i> nota. gram. affixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] des pfp. en <a href="#entry--tavya" class="skt-link">-tavya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तया</span> <i>tayā</i> [i. <a href="#entry-saa_2" class="skt-link">sā_2</a>] ind. par elle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tayos">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तयोस्</span> <i>tayos</i> [g. du. <a href="#entry-sa_2" class="skt-link">sa_2</a>] ind. d'eux deux, d'elles deux — loc. en eux deux, en elles deux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ta_nc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तञ्च्</span> <i>tañc</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(tanakti)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i><i>)</i> se contracter, réduire; coaguler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-te_saam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तेषाम्</span> <i>teṣām</i> [g. pl. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] ind. de ces.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-te_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तेषु</span> <i>teṣu</i> [loc. pl. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] ind. en ces, sur ces.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-te_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ते</span> <i>te_1</i> [nom. pl. <a href="#entry-sa_2" class="skt-link">sa_2</a>] ind. ils; ces — nom. acc. <a href="#entry-te_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> du. elles deux — nom. acc. <a href="#entry-te_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> du. eux deux, ces deux.<br> <i>te</i> <i>te</i> tous ceux-là.<br> <i>te</i> <i>vayam</i> <i>brumaḥ</i> nous-mêmes disons (accord démonstratif <i>te</i> avec pr. 1⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ pers.).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-te_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ते</span> <i>te_2</i> cl. pour <i>tubhyam</i>, <i>tava</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tebhyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तेभ्यस्</span> <i>tebhyas</i> [dat. pl. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] ind. à ces — abl. de ces.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-teja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तेज</span> <i>teja</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tij" class="skt-link">tij</a>] <a href="#entry-teja" class="skt-link">m.</a> acuité; fait d'être pointu ou aiguisé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tejas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तेजस्</span> <i>tejas</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tij" class="skt-link">tij</a>] <a href="#entry-tejas" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«pointe de la flamme»] flamme, chaleur; effulgence, éclat, splendeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ardeur; énergie vitale, vigueur; fougue; force; force virile, sperme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> force spirituelle, puissance, influence morale; gloire, dignité, majesté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. le Feu, l'une des 9 substances [<a href="#entry-dravya_1" class="skt-link">dravya_1</a>] du <a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>, l'un des 5 éléments [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>] du <a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>; son <a href="#entry-pa_ncabhuutasthala" class="skt-link">pañcabhūtasthala</a> est <a href="#entry-Uaru_naacalezvara" class="skt-link">Aruṇācaleśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tejasvin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तेजस्विन्</span> <i>tejasvin</i> [<a href="#entry-tejas" class="skt-link">tejas</a><a href="#entry--vin" class="skt-link">-vin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tejasvin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tejasvin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tejasvinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tejasvinī</i> brillant, splendide, éclatant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> puissant, énergique; courageux; majestueux, noble.<br> <i>tejasvi</i> <i>nāvadhītamastu</i> Fasse que notre étude s'avère efficace (notre au duel: maître et élève).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tejo">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तेजो</span> <i>tejo</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-tejas" class="skt-link">tejas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tejolifga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tejoliṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-tejolifga" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. le <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> de feu, forme ardente de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> découvrant une colonne de feu décidèrent de chercher ses extrémités, le premier volant sur son cygne <a href="#entry-Uha_msa" class="skt-link">Haṃsa</a>, le second plongeant sous la forme du sanglier <a href="#entry-Uvaraaha" class="skt-link">Varāha</a>; ils ne purent, la colonne étant infinie, et rebroussèrent chemin pour se rencontrer; <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> mentit et déclara avoir atteint le sommet, sur la foi de la fleur <a href="#entry-ketakii" class="skt-link">ketakī</a> qui prétendait descendre de la tête de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; celui-ci se révéla devant eux comme <a href="#entry-Uaru_naacalezvara" class="skt-link">Aruṇācaleśvara</a>; il leur accorda un vœu à chacun; <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> demanda à avoir <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> comme fils; outré par cette outrecuidance, <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> décréta qu'il ne serait pas l'objet d'un culte sur Terre; <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> demanda à être serviteur aux pieds de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; il obtint ainsi de s'incarner en <a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tejo" class="skt-link">tejo</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tejomaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tejomaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tejomaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tejomaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tejomayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tejomayī</i> phil. fait d'énergie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tejomayaananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tejomayānanda</i> [<a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a>] <a href="#entry-tejomayaananda" class="skt-link">m.</a> mod. np. du <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a> Tejomayānanda «Béatitude de pure énergie», disciple de <a href="#entry-Ucinmayaananda" class="skt-link">Cinmayānanda</a>; il dirige la «Cinmaya Mission».<br> <a href="#entry-tejomaya" class="skt-link">tejomaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tena">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तेन</span> <i>tena</i> [i. <a href="#entry-tad" class="skt-link">tad</a>] ind. par ce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ti_s_thadgu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tiṣṭhadgu</i> [<a href="#entry-gu" class="skt-link">gu</a>] adv. à l'heure de la traite des vaches (où elles sont debout).<br> <a href="#entry-ti_s_that" class="skt-link">tiṣṭhat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ti_s_that">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिष्ठत्</span> <i>tiṣṭhat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-sthaa_1" class="skt-link">sthā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ti_s_that" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ti_s_that" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ti_s_thantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tiṣṭhantī</i> qui se tient, qui est debout; qui attend, qui dure, qui persiste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ti_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिष्य</span> <i>tiṣya</i> <a href="#entry-ti_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Tiṣya, archer céleste [<a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uk_rzaanu" class="skt-link">Kṛśānu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. son symbole cosmique, le <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a> <a href="#entry-Upu_sya" class="skt-link">Puṣya</a> le Cancer — <a href="#entry-ti_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. le <a href="#entry-kaliyuga" class="skt-link">kaliyuga</a>, âge actuel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ti_syapunarvasu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tiṣyapunarvasu</i> [<a href="#entry-punarvasu" class="skt-link">punarvasu</a>] <a href="#entry-ti_syapunarvasu" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. <i>tiṣyapunarvasū</i> astr. les deux maisons lunaires [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>] <a href="#entry-Uti_sya" class="skt-link">Tiṣya</a> et <a href="#entry-Upunarvasuu" class="skt-link">Punarvasū</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ti_sya" class="skt-link">tiṣya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tif">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिङ्</span> <i>tiṅ</i> nota. gram. désinence personnelle; suffixe [<a href="#entry-pratyaya" class="skt-link">pratyaya</a>] de conjugaison.<br> <i>ekatiṅ</i> <i>vākyam</i> gram. la phrase comporte une forme verbale conjuguée unique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tifanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tiṅanta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] <a href="#entry-tifanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. forme conjuguée finie d'un verbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tifvibhakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tiṅvibhakti</i> [<a href="#entry-vibhakti" class="skt-link">vibhakti</a>] <a href="#entry-tifvibhakti" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. désinence personnelle.<br> <a href="#entry-tif" class="skt-link">tiṅ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiik_s_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तीक्ष्ण</span> <i>tīkṣṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-tij" class="skt-link">tij</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tiik_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tiik_s_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tiik_s_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tīkṣṇā</i> aiguisé; aigu, piquant, perçant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> brûlant, cuisant; épicé; acide; corrosif, virulent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiir_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तीर्ण</span> <i>tīrṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tiir_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tiir_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tiir_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tīrṇā</i> traversé, franchi; atteint, réalisé, accompli <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a traversé, franchi, surmonté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तीर</span> <i>tīra</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] <a href="#entry-tiira" class="skt-link">n.</a> berge, bordure, rive, rivage.<br> <i>bhāgīrathītīre</i> sur les berges de la <a href="#entry-Ubhaagiirathii" class="skt-link">Bhāgīrathī</a>.<br> <i>samudratīre</i> au bord de la mer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiirtha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तीर्थ</span> <i>tīrtha</i> [<a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a><a href="#entry--tha" class="skt-link">-tha</a>] <a href="#entry-tiirtha" class="skt-link">n.</a> gué, descente vers la rivière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> moment ou lieu propice, occasion favorable; direction, instruction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> baignade sacrée, lieu de pélerinage, lieu saint — <a href="#entry-tiirtha" class="skt-link">m.</a> ifc. soc. «qui fait accéder à», titre de renonçant de la tradition dualiste [<a href="#entry-dvaita" class="skt-link">dvaita</a>] de <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a>, pontife du <a href="#entry-matha" class="skt-link">matha</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uu_dupii" class="skt-link">Uḍupī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaanandatiirtha" class="skt-link">Ānandatīrtha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uak_sobhyatiirtha" class="skt-link">Akṣobhyatīrtha</a>, <a href="#entry-Ujayatiirtha" class="skt-link">Jayatīrtha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvyaasatiirtha" class="skt-link">Vyāsatīrtha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiirthadarzana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tīrthadarśana</i> [<a href="#entry-darzana" class="skt-link">darśana</a>] <a href="#entry-tiirthadarzana" class="skt-link">n.</a> pélerinage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiirthafkara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tīrthaṅkara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. [«qui a trouvé le gué du renoncement au travers du flot du <a href="#entry-sa_msaara" class="skt-link">saṃsāra</a>»] l'un des 24 <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> ou maîtres jaïns divinisés; la liste traditionnelle comprend: <a href="#entry-Uaadinaatha" class="skt-link">Ādinātha</a> (<a href="#entry-U_r_sabhanaatha" class="skt-link">Ṛṣabhanātha</a>), <a href="#entry-Uajitanaatha" class="skt-link">Ajitanātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Usambhavanaatha" class="skt-link">Saṃbhavanātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uabhinandana" class="skt-link">Abhinandana</a>, <a href="#entry-Usumatinaatha" class="skt-link">Sumatinātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Upadmaprabha" class="skt-link">Padmaprabha</a>, <a href="#entry-Usupaarzvanaatha" class="skt-link">Supārśvanātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Ucandraprabha" class="skt-link">Candraprabha</a>, <a href="#entry-Upu_spadanta" class="skt-link">Puṣpadanta</a> (<a href="#entry-Usuvidhinaatha" class="skt-link">Suvidhinātha</a>), <a href="#entry-Uziitalanaatha" class="skt-link">Śītalanātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzreyaa_msanaatha" class="skt-link">Śreyāṃsanātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaasupuujya" class="skt-link">Vāsupūjya</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvimalanaatha" class="skt-link">Vimalanātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uanantanaatha" class="skt-link">Anantanātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Udharmanaatha" class="skt-link">Dharmanātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzaantinaatha" class="skt-link">Śāntinātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukunthunaatha" class="skt-link">Kunthunātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uaranaatha" class="skt-link">Aranātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Umallinaatha" class="skt-link">Mallinātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Umunisuvrata" class="skt-link">Munisuvrata</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaminaatha" class="skt-link">Naminātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uneminaatha" class="skt-link">Neminātha</a> (<a href="#entry-Uari_s_tanemi" class="skt-link">Ariṣṭanemi</a>), <a href="#entry-Upaarzvanaatha" class="skt-link">Pārśvanātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvardhamaana" class="skt-link">Vardhamāna</a> <a href="#entry-Umahaaviira_1" class="skt-link">Mahāvīra_1</a>; les deux derniers sont des personnages historiques, les 22 premiers sont sans doute mythiques <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>tīrthakara</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiirtharaaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tīrtharāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-tiirtharaaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> «Roi des lieux de pélerinage», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uprayaaga" class="skt-link">Prayāga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiirthasnaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tīrthasnāna</i> [<a href="#entry-snaana" class="skt-link">snāna</a>] <a href="#entry-tiirthasnaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> ablutions rituelles sur un lieu de pélerinage.<br> <a href="#entry-tiirtha" class="skt-link">tīrtha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiirthasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tīrthasthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaana" class="skt-link">sthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-tiirthasthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lieu de pélerinage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiirthayaatraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tīrthayātrā</i> [<a href="#entry-yaatraa" class="skt-link">yātrā</a>] <a href="#entry-tiirthayaatraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pélerinage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiirtvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तीर्त्वा</span> <i>tīrtvā</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] ind. ayant traversé, ayant atteint, ayant accompli.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiisa_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तीसट</span> <i>tīsaṭa</i> <a href="#entry-tiisa_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Tīsaṭa, médecin du 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle il est fils de <a href="#entry-Uvaagbha_ta_1" class="skt-link">Vāgbhaṭa_1</a> et auteur de la <a href="#entry-Ucikitsaakalikaa" class="skt-link">Cikitsākalikā</a>, dont son fils <a href="#entry-Ucandra_ta" class="skt-link">Candraṭa</a> fit le commentaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiivra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तीव्र</span> <i>tīvra</i> a. <a href="#entry-tiivra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tiivra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tiivraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tīvrā</i> intense, ardent, violent; fort, excessif; terrible <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> incisif, pénétrant; sévère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>torvus</i>, <i>protervus</i>.<br> <i>tīvram</i> adv. avec zèle; sévèrement, violemment, terriblement.<br> <i>tīvreṇa</i> <i>tapasā</i> par de sévères pénitences.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiivratapas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tīvratapas</i> [<a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a>] <a href="#entry-tiivratapas" class="skt-link">n.</a> austérités sévères.<br> <a href="#entry-tiivra" class="skt-link">tīvra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tij">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिज्</span> <i>tij</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(tejate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tikta</i><i>)</i> être aigu, être pointu — ca. <i>(tejayati)</i> aiguiser; exciter, inciter — dés. md. <i>(titikṣate)</i> désirer rester ferme; endurer — int. md. <i>(tetikte)</i> aiguiser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. στιγμα; lat. <i>instinctus</i>; all. strechen; ang. stick; fr. instinct, distinct, stigmate.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tikta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिक्त</span> <i>tikta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tij" class="skt-link">tij</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tikta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tikta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tiktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tiktā</i> amer — <a href="#entry-tikta" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] l'amertume, un type de goût [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिल</span> <i>tila</i> <a href="#entry-tila" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Sesamum indicum</i>, sésame, grain de sésame <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. on dit qu'il provient de la sueur de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> grain de beauté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>til</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tilagu_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tilaguḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-gu_da" class="skt-link">guḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-tilagu_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> boulette sucrée aux grains de sésame; cette friandise, dont <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> est friand, est offerte aux visiteurs du Nouvel An [<a href="#entry-makarasafkraanti" class="skt-link">makarasaṃkrānti</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tilaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिलक</span> <i>tilaka</i> [<a href="#entry-tila" class="skt-link">tila</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-tilaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Simplocos racemosa</i>, arbuste aux fleurs jaunâtres; son écorce est utilisée en médecine, et pour faire les marques au front <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> marque (not. de santal, de <i>tilaka</i> ou d'onguent) au front, ornementale ou indiquant la secte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> grain de beauté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. grains de sésame — <a href="#entry-tilakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tilakā</i> ifc. ornement de, le plus beau des.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tilaka_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tilakaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_na" class="skt-link">kaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-tilaka_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> grain de sésame.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tilalipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tilalipa</i> [<a href="#entry-lipa" class="skt-link">lipa</a>] <a href="#entry-tilalipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a> bengali Tilopa, né <a href="#entry-Upraj_naabhadra" class="skt-link">Prajñābhadra</a> (988–1069); après ses études védiques il devint moine bouddhiste, et reçut l'enseignement de <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a>; après 12 ans de dures pratiques il devint accompli [<a href="#entry-mahaasiddha" class="skt-link">mahāsiddha</a>]; on dit qu'il reçut l'initiation <a href="#entry-vajraayana" class="skt-link">vajrāyana</a> directement de <a href="#entry-Uvajradhara" class="skt-link">Vajradhara</a>; il pratiqua le tantrisme avec une <a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a> fille d'un marchand de sésame, fut exclu de son couvent, et devint lui-même meunier en <a href="#entry-Uudyaana" class="skt-link">Udyāna</a>, d'où son épith. de Tilalipa «Meunier de sésame», popularisé comme <a href="#entry-Utilopa" class="skt-link">Tilopa</a>; <a href="#entry-Usamantabhadra" class="skt-link">Samantabhadra</a>-<a href="#entry-Unaaropa" class="skt-link">Nāropa</a> fut son disciple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tilapi_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tilapiṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-pi_n_da" class="skt-link">piṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-tilapi_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tilapi_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> nougat de grains de sésame.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tilottamaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tilottamā</i> [<a href="#entry-uttamaa" class="skt-link">uttamā</a>] <a href="#entry-tilottamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] Tilottamā «Bellissime» [«dont la beauté ne peut être surpassée même d'un iota»]; elle fut créée par <a href="#entry-Uvizvakarmaa" class="skt-link">Viśvakarmā</a> à partir des jolies parties de toutes choses animées et inanimées, puis fut enfantée par <a href="#entry-Upradhaa" class="skt-link">Pradhā</a>, pour créer la zizanie entre <a href="#entry-Usunda" class="skt-link">Sunda</a> et <a href="#entry-Uupasunda" class="skt-link">Upasunda</a> afin qu'ils s'entretuent; on dit aussi que <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> la créa à partir de poussière de diamants, d'où son nom de «(Faite des) meilleures particules»; la veille de son départ sur Terre elle fit ses adieux au <a href="#entry-Udevaloka" class="skt-link">Devaloka</a> où elle fit le tour des dieux pour les honorer; ensorcelé par sa beauté, <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> ne put la quitter du regard, et quatre visages lui poussèrent; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> n'ayant pas assez d'yeux pour la regarder, son visage s'en couvrit de mille.<br> <a href="#entry-tila" class="skt-link">tila</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-timi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिमि</span> <i>timi</i> <a href="#entry-timi" class="skt-link">m.</a> baleine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Timi, poisson de taille fabuleuse — <a href="#entry-timi" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Timi_1, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>, mère des monstres marins.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-timifgila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>timiṃgila</i> [<a href="#entry-gila" class="skt-link">gila</a>] <a href="#entry-timi_mgila" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a> Timiṃgila «qui avale la baleine <a href="#entry-Utimi" class="skt-link">Timi</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-timifgilagila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>timiṃgilagila</i> [<a href="#entry-gila" class="skt-link">gila</a>] <a href="#entry-timi_mgilagila" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du poisson fabuleux Timiṃgilagila «qui avale <a href="#entry-Utimifgila" class="skt-link">Timiṃgila</a>».<br> <a href="#entry-timifgila" class="skt-link">timiṃgila</a> <br> <a href="#entry-timim" class="skt-link">timim</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-timim">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिमिम्</span> <i>timim</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-timi" class="skt-link">timi</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-timira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिमिर</span> <i>timira</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-tamas" class="skt-link">tamas</a>] <a href="#entry-timira" class="skt-link">n.</a> obscurité, ténèbres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tinduka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिन्दुक</span> <i>tinduka</i> <a href="#entry-tinduka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Diospyros malabarica</i>, ébène du Malabar, arbre à l'écorce noire, de croissance lente, utilisé en médecine et en teinture — <a href="#entry-tinduka" class="skt-link">n.</a> son fruit, astringent; il est utilisé pour sa résine, qui sert à colmater les bateaux; syn. <a href="#entry-tumburu" class="skt-link">tumburu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tinti_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिन्तिड</span> <i>tintiḍa</i> <a href="#entry-tinti_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Tamarindus indica</i>, arbre tamarin; syn. <a href="#entry-ci_ncaa" class="skt-link">ciñcā</a> — <a href="#entry-tinti_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tintiḍī</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sauce amère faite avec son fruit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tinti_diika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tintiḍīka</i> [<a href="#entry--iika" class="skt-link">-īka</a>] <a href="#entry-tinti_diika" class="skt-link">m.</a> id. — <a href="#entry-tinti_diika" class="skt-link">n.</a> son fruit (astringent et laxatif) — <a href="#entry-tinti_dikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tintiḍikā</i> arbre tamarin.<br> <a href="#entry-tinti_da" class="skt-link">tintiḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिरस्</span> <i>tiras</i> adv. de travers; transversalement, obliquement — prép. à travers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à part, sans, contre ⟨acc.⟩ — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(tirasyati)</i> disparaître.<br> <i>tiras</i> <i>cittani</i> sans la connaissance.<br> <i>tiro</i> <i>vaśam</i> contre la volonté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tirask_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिरस्कृ</span> <i>tiraskṛ</i> var. <i>tiraḥkṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-tiras" class="skt-link">tiras</a>-<a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(tiraskaroti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tiraskṛta</i><i>)</i> cacher, couvrir; faire disparaître <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blâmer, gronder, injurier ⟨acc.⟩.<br> <i>tiraskṛtya</i> ind. ayant disparu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tirask_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिरस्कृत</span> <i>tiraskṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tirask_r" class="skt-link">tiraskṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tirask_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tirask_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tirask_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tiraskṛtā</i> caché; disparu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blâmé, grondé, injurié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiraskarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिरस्करिन्</span> <i>tiraskarin</i> [<a href="#entry-tirask_r" class="skt-link">tiraskṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-tiraskarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«ce qui dissimule»] rideau; not. rideau de scène — <a href="#entry-tiraskari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tiraskariṇī</i> voile; not. myth. voile magique qui rend invisible, un attribut des nymphes [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tirazci">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिरश्चि</span> <i>tiraści</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-tiryac" class="skt-link">tiryac</a>] ind. en travers.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tirazciina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tiraścīna</i> [<a href="#entry--iina" class="skt-link">-īna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tirazciina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tirazciina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tirazciinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tiraścīnā</i> transversal; horizontal; à travers, en travers.<br> <a href="#entry-tirazci" class="skt-link">tiraści</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tirobhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिरोभाव</span> <i>tirobhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-tirobhuu" class="skt-link">tirobhū</a>] <a href="#entry-tirobhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> disparition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. le pouvoir divin de dissimulation; opp. <a href="#entry-aavirbhaava" class="skt-link">āvirbhāva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a>] l'illusion, l'une des 5 manifestations divines [<a href="#entry-pa_ncak_rtya" class="skt-link">pañcakṛtya</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tirobhuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिरोभू</span> <i>tirobhū</i> [<a href="#entry-tiras" class="skt-link">tiras</a>-<a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(tirobhavati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tirobhūta</i><i>)</i> disparaître — int. <i>(tirobhobavīti)</i> garder secret ⟨i.⟩.<br> <i>tirobhūya</i> ind. ayant caché; ayant disparu.<br> <i>tiraḥ</i> <i>bhūvā</i> ind. étant parti sur le côté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tirodhaa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिरोधा</span> <i>tirodhā_1</i> [<a href="#entry-tiras" class="skt-link">tiras</a>-<a href="#entry-dhaa_1" class="skt-link">dhā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(tirodadhāti)</i> enlever, retirer; cacher, supprimer — pr. md. <i>(tirodhatte)</i> se dissimuler (aux yeux de ⟨abl.⟩); disparaître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tirodhaa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिरोधा</span> <i>tirodhā_2</i> [<a href="#entry-tirodhaa_1" class="skt-link">tirodhā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tirodhaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> dissimulation, secret.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tirodhaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिरोधान</span> <i>tirodhāna</i> [<a href="#entry-tirodhaa_1" class="skt-link">tirodhā_1</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-tirodhaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> dissimulation; disparition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tirohita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tirohita</i> [<a href="#entry-hita_2" class="skt-link">hita_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tirohita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tirohita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tirohitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tirohitā</i> mis de côté; caché, dissimulé; not. au fig. sens caché.<br> <a href="#entry-tirobhuu" class="skt-link">tirobhū</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiryac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिर्यच्</span> <i>tiryac</i> var. <i>tiryañc</i> [<a href="#entry-tiras" class="skt-link">tiras</a>-<a href="#entry-ac" class="skt-link">ac</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tiryac" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tiryac" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tirazcii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tiraścī</i> oblique, de travers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> horizontal — <a href="#entry-tiryac" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tiryac" class="skt-link">n.</a> animal (qui marche à quatre pattes).<br> <i>tiryak</i> adv. de travers, horizontalement, obliquement, de côté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à l'horizontale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tiryajrekhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tiryajrekhā</i> [<a href="#entry-rekhaa" class="skt-link">rekhā</a>] <a href="#entry-tiryajrekhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> math. bisectrice.<br> <a href="#entry-tiryac" class="skt-link">tiryac</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tis_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिसृ</span> <i>tisṛ</i> [f. <a href="#entry-tri" class="skt-link">tri</a>] num. <a href="#entry-tis_r" class="skt-link">*</a> trois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tita_u">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तितउ</span> <i>tita_u</i> <a href="#entry-tita_u" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tita_u" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. crible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-titha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिथ</span> <i>titha</i> <a href="#entry-titha" class="skt-link">m.</a> feu; temps.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tithi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तिथि</span> <i>tithi</i> [<a href="#entry-titha" class="skt-link">titha</a>] <a href="#entry-tithi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tithi" class="skt-link">f.</a> astr. jour lunaire, 30⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ partie d'une lunaison; il y en a 15 dans chaque demi-lunaison [<a href="#entry-pak_sa" class="skt-link">pakṣa</a>]; les jours favorables sont <a href="#entry-nandaa" class="skt-link">nandā</a>, <a href="#entry-bhadraa" class="skt-link">bhadrā</a>, <a href="#entry-vijayaa" class="skt-link">vijayā</a> et <a href="#entry-puur_naa" class="skt-link">pūrṇā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 15 — <a href="#entry-tithii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tithī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tithik_saya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tithikṣaya</i> [<a href="#entry-k_saya_2" class="skt-link">kṣaya_2</a>] <a href="#entry-tithik_saya" class="skt-link">m.</a> jour de nouvelle lune.<br> <a href="#entry-tithi" class="skt-link">tithi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-titiir_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तितीर्ष्</span> <i>titīrṣ</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-t_rr" class="skt-link">tṝ</a>] v. pr. <i>(titīrṣati)</i> pr. md. <i>(titīrṣate)</i> désirer traverser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-titiir_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तितीर्षु</span> <i>titīrṣu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-titiir_s" class="skt-link">titīrṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-titiir_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-titiir_su" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-titiir_su" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui désire traverser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-titik_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तितिक्ष्</span> <i>titikṣ</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-tij" class="skt-link">tij</a>] v. pr. md. <i>(titikṣate)</i> supporter patiemment, endurer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-titik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तितिक्षा</span> <i>titikṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-titik_s" class="skt-link">titikṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-titik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> endurance, patience <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] la longanimité, l'une des 6 vertus cardinales [<a href="#entry-_sa_tsampad" class="skt-link">ṣaṭsampad</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Titikṣā, la Patience personnifiée, fille de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Uk_sema" class="skt-link">Kṣema</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-titik_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तितिक्षु</span> <i>titikṣu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-titik_s" class="skt-link">titikṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-titik_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-titik_su" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-titik_su" class="skt-link">f.</a> endurant, patient.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tittira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तित्तिर</span> <i>tittira</i> [onomatopée] <a href="#entry-tittira" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. perdrix <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>tittiri</i> natu. id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Tittiri «Perdrix», élève de <a href="#entry-Uyaaska" class="skt-link">Yāska</a>, fondateur présumé de l'école <a href="#entry-taittiriiya" class="skt-link">taittirīya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tittirikalmaa_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tittirikalmāṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-kalmaa_sa" class="skt-link">kalmāṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tittirikalmaa_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tittirikalmaa_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tittirikalmaa_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tittirikalmāṣī</i> se dit d'un cheval à la robe tachetée.<br> <a href="#entry-tittira" class="skt-link">tittira</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्मन्</span> <i>tman</i> ifc. <a href="#entry-tman" class="skt-link">m.</a> [<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] (après ‘<i>e</i>’, ‘<i>o</i>’) (rare); cf. <a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-to_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोष्</span> <i>toṣ</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-tu_s" class="skt-link">tuṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(toṣayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>toṣita</i><i>)</i> plaire, satisfaire; réjouir, contenter; rassurer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-to_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोष</span> <i>toṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-to_s" class="skt-link">toṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-to_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> plaisir, satisfaction, joie (en ⟨loc. g. iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-to_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोषण</span> <i>toṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-to_sa" class="skt-link">toṣa</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] a. <a href="#entry-to_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-to_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-to_sa_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>toṣaṇī</i> plaisant, satisfaisant, réjouissant, réconfortant — <a href="#entry-to_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de réjouir ou de plaire; satisfaction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-to_sala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोषल</span> <i>toṣala</i> <a href="#entry-to_sala" class="skt-link">n.</a> massue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cf. <a href="#entry-tosala" class="skt-link">tosala</a> — <a href="#entry-to_salii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>toṣalī</i> géo. np. de Toṣalī, capitale du <a href="#entry-Ukalifga" class="skt-link">Kaliṅga</a> sous l'empire <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a>; ce centre bd. serait devenu plus tard <a href="#entry-Uzizupaalagaarha" class="skt-link">Śiśupālagārha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-to_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोषिन्</span> <i>toṣin</i> [<a href="#entry-to_sa" class="skt-link">toṣa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-to_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-to_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-to_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>toṣiṇī</i> satisfait de; qui aime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-to_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोषित</span> <i>toṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-to_s" class="skt-link">toṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-to_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-to_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-to_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>toṣitā</i> satisfait; réjoui.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-todana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोदन</span> <i>todana</i> [<a href="#entry-tud_1" class="skt-link">tud_1</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-todana" class="skt-link">n.</a> aiguillon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tol">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोल्</span> <i>tol</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tul" class="skt-link">tul</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tola">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोल</span> <i>tola</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tul" class="skt-link">tul</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tola" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tola" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tolaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tolā</i> qui se mesure — <a href="#entry-tola" class="skt-link">m.</a> école.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tolaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोलक</span> <i>tolaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tul" class="skt-link">tul</a>] <a href="#entry-tolaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tolaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> mesure de poids d'or, valant 16 ou 12 <a href="#entry-maa_sa" class="skt-link">māṣa</a> (environ 25 g).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tolana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोलन</span> <i>tolana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tul" class="skt-link">tul</a>] <a href="#entry-tolana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de soulever, de soupeser; pesage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tolya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोल्य</span> <i>tolya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-tul" class="skt-link">tul</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tolya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tolya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tolyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tolyā</i> à peser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tomara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोमर</span> <i>tomara</i> <a href="#entry-tomara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tomara" class="skt-link">n.</a> javelot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tora_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोरण</span> <i>toraṇa</i> <a href="#entry-tora_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> arch. arc, portail, porche, portique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-toramaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोरमाण</span> <i>toramāṇa</i> <a href="#entry-toramaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi <a href="#entry-huu_na" class="skt-link">hūṇa</a> Toramāṇa (début du 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); son fils <a href="#entry-Umihirakula" class="skt-link">Mihirakula</a> lui succéda.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tosala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोसल</span> <i>tosala</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-to_sala" class="skt-link">toṣala</a>] <a href="#entry-tosala" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. du lutteur Tosala <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. peuple <a href="#entry-Utosala" class="skt-link">Tosala</a>, et son pays, au sud du <a href="#entry-Ukosala" class="skt-link">Kosala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tottra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोत्त्र</span> <i>tottra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-tud_1" class="skt-link">tud_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tottra" class="skt-link">n.</a> aiguillon à conduire le bétail, croc à diriger un éléphant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-toya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तोय</span> <i>toya</i> <a href="#entry-toya" class="skt-link">n.</a> eau.<br> <i>toyaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> faire des libations d'eau aux mânes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्र</span> <i>tra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-trai" class="skt-link">trai</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-tra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-traa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trā</i> qui protège (obj. ⟨iic.⟩).<br> <i>pārṣṇitra</i> n. protège-dos.<br> <i>aṅgulitra</i> n. [«protège-doigts»] gant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रा</span> <i>trā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-trai" class="skt-link">trai</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्राण</span> <i>trāṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-trai" class="skt-link">trai</a>] <a href="#entry-traa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> protection, sauvegarde, préservation, défense; armure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> refuge, secours, assistance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traa_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्राटक</span> <i>trāṭaka</i> <a href="#entry-traa_taka" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] fixation visuelle d'un objet, concentration oculaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traaman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रामन्</span> <i>trāman</i> [<a href="#entry-trai" class="skt-link">trai</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] a. <a href="#entry-traaman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-traaman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-traama_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trāmaṇī</i> protecteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रास</span> <i>trāsa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tras" class="skt-link">tras</a>] <a href="#entry-traasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> tremblement, crainte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रात</span> <i>trāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-trai" class="skt-link">trai</a>] a. <a href="#entry-traata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-traata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-traataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trātā</i> protégé — <a href="#entry-traata" class="skt-link">n.</a> protection.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trai">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रै</span> <i>trai</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(trāyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>trāta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> défendre, protéger, préserver de ⟨abl.⟩; sauver, sauvegarder.<br> <i>svalpamapyasya</i> <i>dharmasya</i> <i>trāyate</i> <i>mahato</i> <i>bhayāt</i> [Bh.G.] Même un petit peu de cet enseignement nous sauve de grands périls.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traigu_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रैगुण्य</span> <i>traiguṇya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-trigu_na" class="skt-link">triguṇa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-traigu_nya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-traigu_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-traigu_nyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>traiguṇyā</i> composé des 3 essences [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>]; constitué de 3 brins — <a href="#entry-traigu_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'être triple, d'avoir 3 qualités <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] la triade des trois essences.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रैक</span> <i>traika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-trika" class="skt-link">trika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-traika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-traika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-traikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>traikā</i> phil. relatif au système de la Triade [<a href="#entry-trika" class="skt-link">trika</a>] du shivaïsme non dual [<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a>] du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trailokya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रैलोक्य</span> <i>trailokya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-triloka" class="skt-link">triloka</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-trailokya" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. l'ensemble des trois mondes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trairaazika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रैराशिक</span> <i>trairāśika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-triraazi" class="skt-link">trirāśi</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-trairaazika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-trairaazika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-trairaazikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trairāśikā</i> concernant 3 nombres — <a href="#entry-trairaazika" class="skt-link">m.</a> math. règle de trois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traivar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रैवर्ण</span> <i>traivarṇa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-trivar_na" class="skt-link">trivarṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-traivar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. membre de l'une des trois classes supérieures: <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a> et <a href="#entry-vaizya" class="skt-link">vaiśya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traivar_nika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>traivarṇika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-traivar_nika" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. id.<br> <a href="#entry-traivar_na" class="skt-link">traivarṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traividya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रैविद्य</span> <i>traividya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-trividya" class="skt-link">trividya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-traividya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-traividya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-traividyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>traividyā</i> soc. qui étudie les 3 Védas — <a href="#entry-traividya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. ensemble des 3 Védas <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. assemblée de brâhmanes connaissant les 3 Védas.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traividyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>traividyaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-traividyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-traividyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-traividyikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>traividyikā</i> soc. pratiqué par les brâhmanes connaissant les 3 Védas.<br> <a href="#entry-traividya" class="skt-link">traividya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traiyambaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रैयम्बक</span> <i>traiyambaka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tryambaka" class="skt-link">tryambaka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-traiyambaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-traiyambaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-traiyambakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>traiyambakā</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Utryambaka" class="skt-link">Tryambaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traiyambakamantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>traiyambakamantra</i> [<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] <a href="#entry-traiyambakamantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> formule dévotionnelle shivaïte; cf. <a href="#entry-tryambaka" class="skt-link">tryambaka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-traiyambaka" class="skt-link">traiyambaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रप्</span> <i>trap</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(trapate)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>apa</i><i>)</i> être intimidé, avoir honte — ca. <i>(trapayati)</i> intimider.<br> <i>trapate</i> il a honte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trapaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रपा</span> <i>trapā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-trap" class="skt-link">trap</a>] <a href="#entry-trapaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pudeur, modestie, timidité; honte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trapu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रपु</span> <i>trapu</i> <a href="#entry-trapu" class="skt-link">n.</a> étain.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रस्</span> <i>tras</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(trasyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>trasta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> trembler, avoir peur de ⟨abl. g.⟩; s'effrayer — ca. <i>(trāsayati)</i> effrayer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. τρεω; lat. <i>terreo</i>; fr. terreur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रस</span> <i>trasa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tras" class="skt-link">tras</a>] a. <a href="#entry-trasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-trasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-trasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trasā</i> qui bouge, qui tremble — <a href="#entry-trasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> (collectif) le règne animal; opp. <a href="#entry-sthaavara" class="skt-link">sthāvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trasadasyu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>trasadasyu</i> [<a href="#entry-dasyu" class="skt-link">dasyu</a>] <a href="#entry-trasadasyu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Trasadasyu «devant qui tremblent les barbares», fils de <a href="#entry-Upurukutsa" class="skt-link">Purukutsa</a> de la lignée solaire [<a href="#entry-suuryava_mza" class="skt-link">sūryavaṃśa</a>]; il est père d'<a href="#entry-Uanara_nya" class="skt-link">Anaraṇya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-trasa" class="skt-link">trasa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trasnu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रस्नु</span> <i>trasnu</i> [<a href="#entry-tras" class="skt-link">tras</a><a href="#entry--nu" class="skt-link">-nu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-trasnu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-trasnu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-trasnu" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«qui tremble»] timide, effrayé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trasta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रस्त</span> <i>trasta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tras" class="skt-link">tras</a>] a. <a href="#entry-trasta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-trasta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-trastaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trastā</i> effrayé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रय</span> <i>traya</i> [<a href="#entry-tri" class="skt-link">tri</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-traya" class="skt-link">n.</a> ensemble de trois, triade, trilogie — <a href="#entry-trayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trayī</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. le triple <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> l'étude du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-traya_h_sa_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>trayaḥṣaṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">ṣaṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-traya_h_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> 63; syn. <a href="#entry-tri_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">triṣaṣṭi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trayas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रयस्</span> <i>trayas</i> [pl. <a href="#entry-tri" class="skt-link">tri</a>] num. <a href="#entry-trayas" class="skt-link">*</a> forme des num. composés avec les décades (23,33, etc.).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trayastri_mza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>trayastriṃśa</i> [<a href="#entry-tri_mzat" class="skt-link">triṃśat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-trayastri_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-trayastri_mza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-trayastri_mzaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trayastriṃśā</i> ord. 33⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> formé de 33 parties — <a href="#entry-trayastri_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. véd. ensemble des 33 dieux [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] de la cour céleste [<a href="#entry-svarga" class="skt-link">svarga</a>] d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, comprenant les 8 Trésors ou principes naturels [<a href="#entry-vasu" class="skt-link">vasu</a>], les 11 Souffles ou aspects de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> [<a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>], les 12 Radieux ou aspects de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a> [<a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>], plus <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> leur roi et <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a>/<a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> le créateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trayastri_mzat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>trayastriṃśat</i> [<a href="#entry-tri_mzat" class="skt-link">triṃśat</a>] num. <a href="#entry-trayastri_mzat" class="skt-link">f.</a> sg. 33.<br> <a href="#entry-trayas" class="skt-link">trayas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trayazcatvaari_mzat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>trayaścatvāriṃśat</i> [<a href="#entry-catvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">catvāriṃśat</a>] <a href="#entry-trayazcatvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">f.</a> 43.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trayii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रयी</span> <i>trayī</i> iic. f. de <a href="#entry-trayas" class="skt-link">trayas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trayo">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रयो</span> <i>trayo</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-trayas" class="skt-link">trayas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trayodazan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>trayodaśan</i> [<a href="#entry-dazan" class="skt-link">daśan</a>] num. <a href="#entry-trayodazan" class="skt-link">*</a> treize — a. <a href="#entry-trayodazan" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-trayodazan" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-trayodazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trayodaśī</i> treizième — <a href="#entry-trayodazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>trayodaśī</i> 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour d'une demi-lunaison; cf. <a href="#entry-dhanvantari" class="skt-link">dhanvantari</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-trayo" class="skt-link">trayo</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-trayyaaru_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
