    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुदत्</span> <i>tudat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-tud_1" class="skt-link">tud_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tudat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tudat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tudantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tudantī</i> frappant; stimulant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tufga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुङ्ग</span> <i>tuṅga</i> a. <a href="#entry-tufga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tufga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tufgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tuṅgā</i> haut; proéminent; fort — <a href="#entry-tufga" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphant; montagne, pic — <a href="#entry-tufgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tuṅgā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Tuṅgā, qui coule à <a href="#entry-Uz_rfgeri" class="skt-link">Śṛṅgeri</a> au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_ta" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭa</a>, et conflue avec la <a href="#entry-Ubhadraa" class="skt-link">Bhadrā</a> à Mysore pour former la <a href="#entry-Utufgabhadraa" class="skt-link">Tuṅgabhadrā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tufgabhadra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tuṅgabhadra</i> [<a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">bhadra</a>] <a href="#entry-tufgabhadra" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphant rétif — <a href="#entry-tufgabhadraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tuṅgabhadrā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Tuṅgabhadrā, formée à Koodli au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_ta" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭa</a> par la confluence des rivières <a href="#entry-Utufgaa" class="skt-link">Tuṅgā</a> et <a href="#entry-Ubhadraa" class="skt-link">Bhadrā</a>; elle rejoint la <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_naa" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇā</a> à Alampur au Telangana; le riche entre-deux-rives (doab) de leur confluence était contrôlé par la citadelle de <a href="#entry-Uraayacuuru" class="skt-link">Rāyacūru</a> (mod. Raichur).<br> <a href="#entry-tufga" class="skt-link">tuṅga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tufgatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tuṅgatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-tufgatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> hauteur, élévation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tugra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुग्र</span> <i>tugra</i> <a href="#entry-tugra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [RV.] np. du roi Tugra, qui envoya son fils <a href="#entry-Ubhujyu" class="skt-link">Bhujyu</a> en conquête au-delà des mers.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuhina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुहिन</span> <i>tuhina</i> <a href="#entry-tuhina" class="skt-link">n.</a> froid, frimas; gelée, givre; neige, glace <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rosée; brouillard <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. id. — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(tuhinayati)</i> neiger; givrer; couvrir de brouillard ou de rosée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuhinaadri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tuhinādri</i> [<a href="#entry-adri" class="skt-link">adri</a>] <a href="#entry-tuhinaadri" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«montagne de glace»] épith. de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuhinaadrijaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tuhinādrijā</i> [<a href="#entry-jaa" class="skt-link">jā</a>] <a href="#entry-tuhinaadrijaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>, «née dans l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>».<br> <a href="#entry-tuhinaadri" class="skt-link">tuhinādri</a> <br> <a href="#entry-tuhina" class="skt-link">tuhina</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tul">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुल्</span> <i>tul</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(tolayati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(tulayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tulita</i><i>)</i> lever, soulever <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> peser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> comparer, juger, évaluer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> équilibrer, égaler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. τληναι; lat. <i>tuli</i>, <i>tollo</i>; ang. toll.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुला</span> <i>tulā</i> [<a href="#entry-tul" class="skt-link">tul</a>] <a href="#entry-tulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> balance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> équilibre, égalité, ressemblance; mesure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mesure de poids, valant 100 <a href="#entry-pala" class="skt-link">pala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Tulā, signe zodiacal [<a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a>] de la Balance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulaabhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tulābhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaara" class="skt-link">bhāra</a>] <a href="#entry-tulaabhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. rituel de don à un prêtre du poids d'une personne (en grain, en or, ...), pour conjurer un mauvais sort.<br> <a href="#entry-tulaa" class="skt-link">tulā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulaakaaverii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tulākāverī</i> [<a href="#entry-kaaverii" class="skt-link">kāverī</a>] <a href="#entry-tulaakaaverii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. cérémonie d'expiation de fautes par le bain dans la <a href="#entry-Ukaaverii" class="skt-link">Kāverī</a> glacée pendant le mois de <a href="#entry-tulaa" class="skt-link">tulā</a>; par un pélerinage de 3 jours on y acquiert la libération [<a href="#entry-mok_sa" class="skt-link">mokṣa</a>]; myth. le roi <a href="#entry-Uharizcandra" class="skt-link">Hariścandra</a> s'y purifia de la malédiction de <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a> avant d'accomplir son <a href="#entry-azvamedha" class="skt-link">aśvamedha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulaakaaveriimahaatmya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tulākāverīmahātmya</i> [<a href="#entry-mahaatmya" class="skt-link">mahātmya</a>] <a href="#entry-tulaakaaveriimahaatmya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. portion de l'<a href="#entry-Uagnipuraa_na" class="skt-link">Agnipurāṇa</a> décrivant le rite de <a href="#entry-tulaakaaverii" class="skt-link">tulākāverī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tulaakaaverii" class="skt-link">tulākāverī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulaapuru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tulāpuruṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-puru_sa" class="skt-link">puruṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-tulaapuru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> poids de la personne (royale) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. don d'or selon le poids d'une personne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulaapuru_sadaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tulāpuruṣadāna</i> [<a href="#entry-daana" class="skt-link">dāna</a>] <a href="#entry-tulaapuru_sadaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. cérémonie de pesée du roi en or <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. pénitence de donner son poids en or.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulasii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुलसी</span> <i>tulasī</i> <a href="#entry-tulasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Ocymum sanctum</i>, basilic sacré, labiée à fleurs blanches; myth. elle provient du barattage de la mer de lait [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>]; on en fait l'offrande à <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-v_rndaa" class="skt-link">vṛndā</a>; soc. on célèbre l'union de la <a href="#entry-tulasii" class="skt-link">tulasī</a> avec le <a href="#entry-zaalagraama" class="skt-link">śālagrāma</a> par le rite de <i>tulasīśālagrāmavivāha</i> à <a href="#entry-kaarttikapuur_nimaa" class="skt-link">kārttikapūrṇimā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulasiidaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tulasīdāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">dāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-tulasiidaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Tulasīdāsa, poète et philosophe (1532–1623), né à Rajapur (Uttar Pradesh); il vécut à <a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a>, où il écrivit le <a href="#entry-Uraamacaritamaanasa" class="skt-link">Rāmacaritamānasa</a>; on lui doit aussi la <a href="#entry-Ukavitaavali" class="skt-link">Kavitāvali</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tulasii" class="skt-link">tulasī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुलित</span> <i>tulita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tul" class="skt-link">tul</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tulita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tulita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tulitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tulitā</i> soulevé, pesé; comparé; égalé, égal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुल्य</span> <i>tulya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-tul" class="skt-link">tul</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tulya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tulya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tulyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tulyā</i> égal, semblable à ⟨i. g. iic.⟩; en même quantité; aussi cher que ⟨i.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. de la même classe ou caste.<br> <i>deveṣvapi</i> <i>rāmasya</i> <i>tulyo</i> <i>nāsti</i> Même parmi les dieux, <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> n'a pas son égal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulyaak_sara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tulyākṣara</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sara" class="skt-link">akṣara</a>] <a href="#entry-tulyaak_sara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. palindrome; syn. <a href="#entry-samaak_sara" class="skt-link">samākṣara</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tulya" class="skt-link">tulya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tulyatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tulyatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-tulyatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> égalité; ressemblance avec ⟨i. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tumartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुमर्थ</span> <i>tumartha</i> [<a href="#entry--tum" class="skt-link">-tum</a>-<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tumartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tumartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tumarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tumarthā</i> gram. qui a le sens de <a href="#entry--tum" class="skt-link">-tum</a>; qui qualifie le but de l'action — <a href="#entry-tumartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. signification du suffixe <a href="#entry--tum" class="skt-link">-tum</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tumburu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुम्बुरु</span> <i>tumburu</i> <a href="#entry-tumburu" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. graine de coriandre (bot. <i>Coriandrum sativum</i>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. fruit du bot. <i>Diospyros embryopteris</i> (utilisé pour sa résine); syn. <a href="#entry-tinduka" class="skt-link">tinduka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> Tumburu «Tambour de danse»; il était le maître de musique et de danse des nymphes [<a href="#entry-apsarasas" class="skt-link">apsarasas</a>]; sa compagne était <a href="#entry-Udundubhii" class="skt-link">Dundubhī</a>; un jour où la nymphe <a href="#entry-Urambhaa" class="skt-link">Rambhā</a> dansait à la cour d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, <a href="#entry-Upuruuravaa" class="skt-link">Purūravā</a> se moqua d'elle; <a href="#entry-Utumburu" class="skt-link">Tumburu</a> le punit en décrétant sa séparation d'avec <a href="#entry-Uurvazii" class="skt-link">Urvaśī</a> jusqu'à ce qu'il fasse pénitence; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] un jour <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> le requit de lui amener <a href="#entry-Urambhaa" class="skt-link">Rambhā</a>; n'ayant pas réussi dans le temps imparti, <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> le maudit de renaître comme le <a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a> <a href="#entry-Uviraadha" class="skt-link">Virādha</a>; il n'en serait libéré que par le glaive de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tumula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुमुल</span> <i>tumula</i> a. <a href="#entry-tumula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tumula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tumulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tumulā</i> bruyant; tumultueux — <a href="#entry-tumula" class="skt-link">n.</a> tumulte; confusion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>tumultus</i>; fr. tumulte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tumun">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुमुन्</span> <i>tumun</i> nota. gram. affixe primaire [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] de l'infinitif (ex. <i>bhavitum</i>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tunda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुन्द</span> <i>tunda</i> <a href="#entry-tunda" class="skt-link">n.</a> ventre, panse — <a href="#entry-tunda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tundii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tundī</i> nombril — a. <a href="#entry-tunda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tunda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tundii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tundī</i> ventru.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tundaparim_rja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tundaparimṛja</i> [<a href="#entry-parim_rja" class="skt-link">parimṛja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tundaparim_rja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tundaparim_rja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tundaparim_rjaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tundaparimṛjā</i> [«qui se frotte la panse»] paresseux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tundavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tundavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tundavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tundavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tundavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tundavatī</i> corpulent.<br> <a href="#entry-tunda" class="skt-link">tunda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tunna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुन्न</span> <i>tunna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tud_1" class="skt-link">tud_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tunna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tunna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tunnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tunnā</i> poussé, incité, stimulé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tunnavaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tunnavāya</i> [<a href="#entry-vaaya" class="skt-link">vāya</a>] <a href="#entry-tunnavaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> tailleur.<br> <a href="#entry-tunna" class="skt-link">tunna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tup">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुप्</span> <i>tup</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(tupati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(topati)</i> pousser; frapper; tuer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. τυπτω; lat. <i>stupeo</i>; all. stumph; fr. stupeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुर</span> <i>tura</i> a. <a href="#entry-tura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-turaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>turā</i> rapide, prompt.<br> <i>turam</i> adv. rapidement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-turafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुरंग</span> <i>turaṃga</i> var. <i>turaṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-turam" class="skt-link">turam</a>-<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tura_mga" class="skt-link">m.</a> cheval [«qui va vite»] — <a href="#entry-tura_mgii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>turaṃgī</i> jument.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-turaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुरग</span> <i>turaga</i> cf. <a href="#entry-turafga" class="skt-link">turaṃga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-turiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुरीय</span> <i>turīya</i> var. <i>turya</i> [<a href="#entry-catur" class="skt-link">catur</a>] a. <a href="#entry-turiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-turiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-turiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>turīyā</i> ord. quatrième — <a href="#entry-turiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> le quart <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. le 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ stade de l'âme, l'état de libération dans le <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-maa_n_duukyopani_sad" class="skt-link">māṇḍūkyopaniṣad</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-turu_ska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुरुष्क</span> <i>turuṣka</i> <a href="#entry-turu_ska" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>turuṣkās</i> mod. np. des Turuṣkās, les turcs; les musulmans.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-turu_skada_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>turuṣkadaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-da_n_da" class="skt-link">daṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-turu_skada_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. impôt levé par le roi Candradeva de <a href="#entry-Ukanyaakubjaa" class="skt-link">Kanyākubjā</a> (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle) pour se protéger contre les invasions afghanes.<br> <a href="#entry-turu_ska" class="skt-link">turuṣka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-turvasu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुर्वसु</span> <i>turvasu</i> var. <i>turvaśa</i> <a href="#entry-turvasu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du héros Turvasu, fils de <a href="#entry-Uyayaati" class="skt-link">Yayāti</a> et <a href="#entry-Udevayaanii" class="skt-link">Devayānī</a>, frère de <a href="#entry-Uyadu" class="skt-link">Yadu</a> et demi-frère de <a href="#entry-Upuru" class="skt-link">Puru</a>; il reçut en partage l'Occident; les <a href="#entry-yavanaas" class="skt-link">yavanās</a> sont dits ses descendants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-turya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुर्य</span> <i>turya</i> [<a href="#entry-turiiya" class="skt-link">turīya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-turya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-turya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-turyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>turyā</i> formant une 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ part <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cf. <a href="#entry-turiiya" class="skt-link">turīya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-turyagaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>turyagā</i> [<a href="#entry-gaa" class="skt-link">gā</a>] <a href="#entry-turyagaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [YV.] l'accession à l'état de libération dans le <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>, 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ niveau de connaissance [<a href="#entry-j_naanabhuumikaa" class="skt-link">jñānabhūmikā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-turyatama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>turyatama</i> [<a href="#entry--tama" class="skt-link">-tama</a>] <a href="#entry-turyatama" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-Uraama_na" class="skt-link">Rāmaṇa</a>] la libération par fusion de l'âme [<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] avec le <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>, en tant qu'état distingué des stades ordinaires de la conscience [<a href="#entry-avasthaatraya" class="skt-link">avasthātraya</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-turya" class="skt-link">turya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuu_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तूण</span> <i>tūṇa</i> var. <i>tūṇīra</i> <a href="#entry-tuu_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> carquois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuu_s_nii_msthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tūṣṇīṃsthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaana" class="skt-link">sthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-tuu_s_nii_msthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> silence; fait de garder le silence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuu_s_niim">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तूष्णीम्</span> <i>tūṣṇīm</i> [<a href="#entry-tu_s" class="skt-link">tuṣ</a><a href="#entry--niim" class="skt-link">-nīm</a>] adv. tranquillement, silencieusement, en silence; tacitement.<br> <i>tūṣṇīṃ</i> <i>bhū</i> se taire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuu_s_niimbhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tūṣṇīṃbhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">bhāva</a>] <a href="#entry-tuu_s_nii_mbhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> silence; fait de se taire.<br> <a href="#entry-tuu_s_niim" class="skt-link">tūṣṇīm</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तूल</span> <i>tūla</i> <a href="#entry-tuula" class="skt-link">n.</a> touffe d'herbe; grappe; panicule <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> flocon de coton.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuulika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tūlika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-tuulika" class="skt-link">m.</a> marchand de coton — <a href="#entry-tuulikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tūlikā</i> pinceau; matelas de coton.<br> <a href="#entry-tuula" class="skt-link">tūla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuur_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तूर्ण</span> <i>tūrṇa</i> var. <i>tūrta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tvar" class="skt-link">tvar</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tuur_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tuur_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tuur_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tūrṇā</i> rapide <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>tūrta</i> f. id..<br> <i>tūrṇam</i> adv. rapidement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuur_naka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tūrṇaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-tuur_naka" class="skt-link">m.</a> sorte de riz «qui pousse rapidement».<br> <a href="#entry-tuur_na" class="skt-link">tūrṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuur_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तूर्णि</span> <i>tūrṇi</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tvar" class="skt-link">tvar</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tuur_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tuur_ni" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tuur_ni" class="skt-link">f.</a> rapide; intelligent; zélé — <a href="#entry-tuur_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> pensée — <a href="#entry-tuur_ni" class="skt-link">f.</a> vitesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuurta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तूर्त</span> <i>tūrta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tvar" class="skt-link">tvar</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tuurta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tuurta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tuurtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tūrtā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tuur_naa" class="skt-link">tūrṇā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tuurya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तूर्य</span> <i>tūrya</i> <a href="#entry-tuurya" class="skt-link">n.</a> trompette <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> instrument de musique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्व</span> <i>tva</i> pn. <a href="#entry-tva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tva" class="skt-link">f.</a> <a href="#entry-tvad_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>tvad_1</i> véd. un; certain; plusieurs — adv. <i>tvad_2</i> véd. partiellement.<br> <i>tva</i> <i>...</i> <i>tva</i> véd. l'un ... l'autre.<br> <i>uta</i> <i>tvaḥ</i> <i>paśyan</i> <i>na</i> <i>dadarṣa</i> <i>vācam</i> <i>uta</i> <i>tvaḥ</i> <i>śṛṇvan</i> <i>na</i> <i>śṛṇoti</i> <i>enām</i> [RV.] En vérité l'un regarde la Parole sans la voir, et l'autre l'écoute sans l'entendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tva_s_t_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वष्टृ</span> <i>tvaṣṭṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tvak_s" class="skt-link">tvakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tva_s_t_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tva_s_t_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tva_s_trii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tvaṣṭrī</i> charpentier, charron <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. véd. np. de Tvaṣṭā «l'Artisan», radieux [<a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>] inventeur des arts et des techniques; on l'associe à la manifestation [<a href="#entry-aaprii_2" class="skt-link">āprī_2</a>] d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> comme feu plastique; il façonna les êtres avec sa hache; il forgea le foudre [<a href="#entry-vajra" class="skt-link">vajra</a>] d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> avec les os du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-Udadhiici" class="skt-link">Dadhīci</a>; il est considéré comme un géniteur [<a href="#entry-prajaapati" class="skt-link">prajāpati</a>], et souvent confondu avec son fils <a href="#entry-Uvizvakarmaa" class="skt-link">Viśvakarmā</a>; pour combattre <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> qui avait tué son fils synthétique <a href="#entry-Utriziraa" class="skt-link">Triśirā</a>-<a href="#entry-Uvizvaruupa" class="skt-link">Viśvarūpa</a>, il créa l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Uv_rtra" class="skt-link">Vṛtra</a> en offrant en oblation son chignon dans le feu sacrificiel; <a href="#entry-Uracanaa" class="skt-link">Racanā</a> est son épouse, <a href="#entry-Usara_nyuu" class="skt-link">Saraṇyū</a> est sa fille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvaa_s_tra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वाष्ट्र</span> <i>tvāṣṭra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-tva_s_t_r" class="skt-link">tvaṣṭṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tvaa_s_tra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tvaa_s_tra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tvaa_s_traa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tvāṣṭrā</i> myth. relatif à <a href="#entry-Utva_s_taa" class="skt-link">Tvaṣṭā</a> — <a href="#entry-tvaa_s_tra" class="skt-link">n.</a> puissance créatrice — <a href="#entry-tvaa_s_tra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. véd. np. de Tvāṣṭra «fils de <a href="#entry-Utva_s_taa" class="skt-link">Tvaṣṭā</a>», patr. du monstre tricéphale <a href="#entry-Utriziraa" class="skt-link">Triśirā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvizvaruupa" class="skt-link">Viśvarūpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvaad_rz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वादृश्</span> <i>tvādṛś</i> [<a href="#entry-tvad" class="skt-link">tvad</a>-<a href="#entry-d_rz_2" class="skt-link">dṛś_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tvaad_rz" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tvaad_rz" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tvaad_rz" class="skt-link">f.</a> tel que toi, ton pareil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvaam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वाम्</span> <i>tvām</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-tvad" class="skt-link">tvad</a>] ind. te, à toi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वच्</span> <i>tvac</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-tvak_s" class="skt-link">tvakṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-tvac" class="skt-link">f.</a> peau; écorce <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] le sens [<a href="#entry-buddhiindriya" class="skt-link">buddhīndriya</a>] tactil; son régent [<a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">niyantṛ</a>] est <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a>; la perception [<a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">tanmātra</a>] associée est le toucher [<a href="#entry-sparza" class="skt-link">sparśa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvaca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वच</span> <i>tvaca</i> [<a href="#entry-tvac" class="skt-link">tvac</a>] <a href="#entry-tvaca" class="skt-link">n.</a> cannelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वद्</span> <i>tvad</i> pn. <a href="#entry-tvad" class="skt-link">*</a> 2ème pers. tu, vous <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. thou, you; fr. tu, te, toi, vous.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvadiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tvadīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tvadiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tvadiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tvadiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tvadīyā</i> poss. ton, tien, à toi, de toi.<br> <a href="#entry-tvad" class="skt-link">tvad</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvak">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वक्</span> <i>tvak</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tvac" class="skt-link">tvac</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvak_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वक्ष्</span> <i>tvakṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(tvakṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tvaṣṭa</i><i>)</i> fendre (<i>kāṣṭham</i> du bois); dépecer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वम्</span> <i>tvam</i> [nom. <a href="#entry-tvad" class="skt-link">tvad</a>] ind. tu.<br> <i>tvam</i> <i>eva</i> toi seulement.<br> <i>tvaṃ</i> <i>kāra</i> m. tutoiement.<br> <i>tvaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> tutoyer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वर्</span> <i>tvar</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(tvarate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tvarita</i>, <i>tūrṇa</i><i>)</i> se hâter, se dépêcher, être pressé — ca. <i>(tvarayati)</i> faire se presser; précipiter, accélérer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>turba</i>; fr. turbulent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvaraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वरा</span> <i>tvarā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tvar" class="skt-link">tvar</a>] <a href="#entry-tvaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> hâte, empressement; rapidité.<br> <i>tvarāṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> faire vite, se dépêcher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वरित</span> <i>tvarita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tvar" class="skt-link">tvar</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tvarita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tvarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tvaritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tvaritā</i> rapide, pressé — <a href="#entry-tvarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> hâte — <a href="#entry-tvaritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tvaritā</i> myth. np. de Tvaritā, épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> «la Rapide».<br> <i>tvaritam</i> adv. rapidement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvaritaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tvaritaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-tvaritaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tvaritakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tvaritakā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tuur_naka" class="skt-link">tūrṇaka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. de Tvaritaka, suivant du prince <a href="#entry-Ucandraapii_da_1" class="skt-link">Candrāpīḍa_1</a>, qui décrit son état léthagique au roi <a href="#entry-Utaaraapii_da" class="skt-link">Tārāpīḍa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tvarita" class="skt-link">tvarita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvattas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वत्तस्</span> <i>tvattas</i> [abl. <a href="#entry-tvad" class="skt-link">tvad</a>] de toi, de ta part.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वया</span> <i>tvayā</i> [i. <a href="#entry-tvad" class="skt-link">tvad</a>] ind. par toi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvayi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्वयि</span> <i>tvayi</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-tvad" class="skt-link">tvad</a>] ind. en toi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvi_s_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्विष्</span> <i>tviṣ_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(tveṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tviṣita</i><i>)</i> exciter, instiguer — pr. md. <i>(tveṣate)</i> être agité, être troublé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvi_s_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्विष्</span> <i>tviṣ_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tvi_s_1" class="skt-link">tviṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tvi_s_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> agitation; véhémence; furie, violence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> splendeur; beauté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvi_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्विषा</span> <i>tviṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tvi_s_1" class="skt-link">tviṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tvi_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> splendeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tvi_si">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्विषि</span> <i>tviṣi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tvi_s_1" class="skt-link">tviṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tvi_si" class="skt-link">f.</a> véhémence; impétuosité, énergie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> splendeur; beauté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyaaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्याग</span> <i>tyāga</i> [act. <a href="#entry-tyaj_1" class="skt-link">tyaj_1</a>] <a href="#entry-tyaaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> abandon, action de quitter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> renonciation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. détachement du fruit des actes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> don, présent; donation, libéralité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> homme pratiquant le renoncement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyaagaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tyāgarāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raajan" class="skt-link">rājan</a>] <a href="#entry-tyaagaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Tyāgarāja «Roi du renoncement», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; son temple principal se trouve à <a href="#entry-Ukamalayak_setra" class="skt-link">Kamalayakṣetra</a> (mod. Thiruvarur), au Tamil Nadu; il est souvent représenté en <a href="#entry-somaskanda" class="skt-link">somaskanda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-tyaaga" class="skt-link">tyāga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyaagin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्यागिन्</span> <i>tyāgin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tyaj_1" class="skt-link">tyaj_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tyaagin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tyaagin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tyaaginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tyāginī</i> qui abandonne, qui renonce; infidèle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> libéral, généreux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. secte [<a href="#entry-sampradaaya" class="skt-link">sampradāya</a>] de renonçants Tyāgī, condidérée comme une division des <a href="#entry-Uraamaanandii" class="skt-link">Rāmānandī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyaaj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्याज्</span> <i>tyāj</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-tyaj_1" class="skt-link">tyaj_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(tyājayati)</i> faire abandonner; priver de ⟨i.⟩; bannir; évacuer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyaajya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्याज्य</span> <i>tyājya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-tyaj_1" class="skt-link">tyaj_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tyaajya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tyaajya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tyaajyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tyājyā</i> que l'on doit abandonner ou bannir, dont on doit se défaire; que l'on doit sacrifier; se dit not. des actes impurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्यद्</span> <i>tyad</i> pn. 3ème pers. <a href="#entry-tyad" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. ce; le — m. cf. <a href="#entry-sya" class="skt-link">sya</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-syaa" class="skt-link">syā</a>.<br> <i>ha</i> <i>tyad</i> ind. en effet; comme on le sait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyaj_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्यज्</span> <i>tyaj_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(tyajati)</i> pft. <i>(tatyāja)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>tyakta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>tyajanīya</i>, <i>tyājya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>tyaktvā</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> quitter, abandonner, lâcher; sacrifier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> renoncer à; renvoyer, répudier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perdre, laisser se perdre; négliger <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> laisser partir, lancer — ca. <i>(tyājayati)</i> faire abandonner; priver de ⟨i.⟩; bannir; évacuer.<br> <i>rāmo</i> <i>nagaraṃ</i> <i>tyajati</i> <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> quitte la ville.<br> <i>yuddham</i> <i>tyajata</i> Renonçons à la guerre.<br> <i>tyajedekaṃ</i> <i>kulasyārthe</i> <i>grāmasyārthe</i> <i>kulaṃ</i> <i>tyajet</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>grāmaṃ</i> <i>janapadasyārthe</i> <i>svātmārthe</i> <i>pṛthivīṃ</i> <i>tyajet</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [PT.] Sacrifie un homme pour sauver la famille, sacrifie une famille pour sauver le village, sacrifie un village pour sauver le pays, sacrifie la Terre pour te sauver.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyaj_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्यज्</span> <i>tyaj_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-tyaj_1" class="skt-link">tyaj_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-tyaj_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tyaj_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tyaj_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui renonce à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्यज</span> <i>tyaja</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tyajya" class="skt-link">tyajya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyajaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्यजनीय</span> <i>tyajanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-tyaj_1" class="skt-link">tyaj_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tyajaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tyajaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tyajaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tyajanīyā</i> à abandonner, dont on doit se défaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyajya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्यज्य</span> <i>tyajya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-tyaj_1" class="skt-link">tyaj_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tyajya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tyajya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tyajyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tyajyā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-tyaajya" class="skt-link">tyājya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyakta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्यक्त</span> <i>tyakta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-tyaj_1" class="skt-link">tyaj_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tyakta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tyakta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tyaktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tyaktā</i> quitté, abandonné, lâché, rejeté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyaktajiivita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>tyaktajīvita</i> [<a href="#entry-jiivita" class="skt-link">jīvita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-tyaktajiivita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-tyaktajiivita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-tyaktajiivitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>tyaktajīvitā</i> qui a renoncé à la vie; prêt à mourir; qui donne sa vie pour ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <a href="#entry-tyakta" class="skt-link">tyakta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-tyaktvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्यक्त्वा</span> <i>tyaktvā</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-tyaj_1" class="skt-link">tyaj_1</a>] ind. ayant quitté; ayant abandonné.
  </div>
</div>
